{"id":"49f89cc1-2839-4b3c-b9c7-1b3da84c6e7a","identifier":"ENT-297","title":"Subagent file modifications invisible to checkpoint detection","description":"## Problem\n\n`ExtractModifiedFiles` (`agent/claudecode/transcript.go`) only parses the **main agent transcript** for Write/Edit/NotebookEdit tool_use blocks. When subagents (via the `Task` tool) perform file modifications, those tool_use entries live in **separate subagent transcript files** — making them completely invisible to the detection logic.\n\n### Impact\n\n1. **Missing checkpoints (root cause of** [ENT-287](https://linear.app/entirehq/issue/ENT-287/checkpoint-trailer-added-but-checkpoints-not-created-locally-on) **/ GitHub #274)**: In the Stop hook (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:~254`), `totalChanges` is composed of:\n   * `relModifiedFiles` — from transcript parsing (`ExtractModifiedFiles`)\n   * `relNewFiles` / `relDeletedFiles` — from untracked file comparison (`DetectFileChanges`)\n\n   Modifications to **tracked** files are entirely transcript-derived. When subagents do the work, the main transcript only contains `Task` tool calls (not Write/Edit) → `totalChanges = 0` → `SaveChanges` skipped → no shadow branch created → no condensation → **no checkpoint persisted**.\n2. **Misattribution**: Subagent file changes are not attributed to the agent — they appear as \"human\" edits since they're invisible to the transcript-based detection.\n\n### Why this wasn't caught in tests\n\nAll existing tests (e.g., `phase_postcommit_test.go:852`, `phase_transitions_test.go:74`) call `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` or `SimulateStop` before mid-session commits, ensuring the shadow branch always exists. No test exercises the path where only subagents modify files.\n\n### Reproduction\n\n1. Start a session where the main agent delegates all file modifications to subagents (Task tool)\n2. The subagents call Write/Edit to modify tracked files\n3. Stop the session — observe `totalChanges = 0`, \"No files were modified\" message\n4. Make a commit — no `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer, no condensation\n\n### Where the bug lives\n\n| File | Function | Issue |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `agent/claudecode/transcript.go` | `ExtractModifiedFiles` | Only parses main transcript JSONL |\n| `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:~218` | `commitWithMetadata` | `modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)` — only main transcript |\n| `state.go` | `DetectFileChanges` | Only handles new/deleted **untracked** files, not modifications to tracked files |\n| `manual_commit_hooks.go:~1049` | `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` | Same `ExtractModifiedFiles` blind spot |\n\n### Possible approaches\n\n1. **Parse subagent transcripts too**: `ExtractModifiedFiles` could also scan `.entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/*/agent-*.jsonl` files for Write/Edit tool calls\n2. **Filesystem-based detection**: Compare working tree against HEAD (or pre-prompt snapshot) to detect all modifications regardless of source — more robust but different Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive summary.

## Comprehensive Analysis: Checkpoint Detection Blind Spot for Subagent File Modifications

I've completed a thorough exploration of the checkpoint detection pipeline. Here's what I found:

### DETECTION PIPELINE OVERVIEW

The Stop hook (`commitWithMetadata` in `/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`) follows this detection sequence:

1. **Parse the main transcript** (line 189-205)
   - Reads from `transcriptPath` (the **main Claude Code agent's** transcript)
   - Extracts lines from `transcriptOffset` onwards

2. **Extract modified files from parsed transcript** (line 225)
   - Calls `extractModifiedFiles(transcript)` on the parsed main transcript
   - `extractModifiedFiles` scans for `assistant` messages with tool_use blocks
   - Checks if the tool is in `claudecode.FileModificationTools`
   - Extracts file paths from the tool's input

3. **Detect git status changes** (line 247)
   - Calls `DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())`
   - Uses `git status` to detect Modified, New, and Deleted files

4. **Combine and normalize paths** (line 253-258)
   - Filters transcript-derived files and git status through `FilterAndNormalizePaths`
   - Converts absolute → relative paths, excludes infrastructure files

5. **Gate on change count** (line 261-272)
   - Only commits if `totalChanges > 0`
   - If no changes detected, skips commit and exits

### THE CRITICAL BLIND SPOT

**The main transcript only contains references to tool calls, not the actual subagent work.**

When Claude spawns a subagent via the `Task` tool:

1. Main transcript logs:
   - `assistant`: Tool use block with `Task` tool + instructions
   - `user`: Tool result with response text (e.g., "agentId: xyz123")

2. **Subagent's actual work** is in a separate transcript file:
   - Stored at: `.claude-code/[sessionID]/subagents/agent-{agentID}.jsonl`
   - Contains the subagent's tool use blocks (file modifications)
   - **NEVER scanned by the Stop hook**

3. Current flow in `extractModifiedFiles(main_transcript)`:
   - Only sees the `Task` tool call in the main transcript
   - Never reads the subagent transcript file
   - Subagent's `Read`, `Write`, `Str Replace` calls are invisible

### DETECTION METHODS COMPARED

| Detection Method | Reads From | Finds |
|---|---|---|
| **extractModifiedFiles** (main hook) | Main transcript tool_use blocks | Only main agent's file tools |
| **extractModifiedFiles** (PostTask hook) | Subagent transcript (if provided) | Subagent's actual file modifications |
| **DetectFileChanges** (git status) | Worktree state only | Files that actually exist on disk |

**The gap:** If a subagent modifies files but doesn't create commits, git status captures the changes, but the Stop hook fails to attribute them correctly because:
1. The modified files aren't in the main transcript (they're in the subagent transcript)
2. When `extractModifiedFiles(main_transcript)` returns empty, the Stop hook only has git status data
3. Git status shows files changed, but the Stop hook doesn't correlate them with subagent work

### KEY FILES IN THE PIPELINE

**Transcript Extraction:**
- `/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go` (line 63-114)
  - `ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)` - Scans transcript lines for tool modifications
  - Only processes `assistant` messages with `FileModificationTools`
  - Returns file paths from `FilePath` or `NotebookPath` fields

- `/cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go` (line 332-376)
  - `extractModifiedFiles(transcript)` - Local CLI wrapper
  - Identical logic to Claude Code's version

**File Change Detection:**
- `/cmd/entire/cli/state.go` (line 242-291)
  - `DetectFileChanges()` - Reads git status
  - Categorizes: Modified (worktree/staging), New (untracked), Deleted
  - Filters out `.entire/` infrastructure paths

**Stop Hook Orchestration:**
- `/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (line 97-396)
  - `commitWithMetadata()` - Main Stop hook
  - Lines 225: Extract modified files from **main** transcript only
  - Lines 247: Detect git status
  - Lines 253-258: Combine both sources
  - Lines 261-272: Gate on `totalChanges == 0` → skip commit if empty

**Task Checkpoint Handling:**
- `/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (line 555-696)
  - `handleClaudeCodePostTask()` - Task completion hook
  - Lines 593-611: Correctly reads both main and subagent transcripts
  - Uses `SubagentTranscriptPath` to extract subagent modifications
  - Creates separate task checkpoints

### WHERE THE BLIND SPOT MANIFESTS

**Scenario:** Subagent creates/modifies files without the main agent creating commits

1. **At PostTask time:** `handleClaudeCodePostTask` (line 688)
   - Calls `SaveTaskCheckpoint()`
   - Correctly reads subagent transcript
   - Task checkpoint created with subagent's files

2. **At Stop time:** `commitWithMetadata` (line 225-226)
   ```go
   modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)  // Only main transcript
   // ^ Subagent files NOT included
   ```
   - Main transcript has no tool_use for the files (subagent modified them)
   - Git status DOES show the modified files
   - But the Stop hook doesn't correlate them with subagent work

3. **Result:**
   - Files appear in git diff but aren't annotated as "modified via subagent"
   - Session checkpoint lacks the transcript data proving subagent did the work
   - Rewind won't have subagent tool calls in the metadata

### INFORMATION FLOW GAPS

**What exists:**
- Subagent transcripts: `.claude-code/[sessionID]/subagents/agent-{id}.jsonl`
- Subagent tool calls: Recorded in separate transcript files
- Task metadata: Stored under `.git/entire-metadata/[sessionID]/tasks/[toolUseID]/`

**What's missing in Stop hook:**
1. No code to read/scan subagent transcript files
2. No extraction of `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()` from main transcript
3. No lookup of subagent transcripts based on detected agent IDs
4. No merging of subagent-modified files into the Stop hook's file list

**What the Stop hook should do (but doesn't):**

```go
// After extracting from main transcript:
modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)

// Also extract subagent files:
agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)  // Available in claudecode package
for agentID := range agentIDs {
    subagentTranscriptPath := AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, agentID)
    if fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
        subagentTranscript, _ := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
        subagentFiles := extractModifiedFiles(subagentTranscript)
        modifiedFiles = append(modifiedFiles, subagentFiles...)
    }
}
```

This code exists in `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` (claudecode/transcript.go, lines 387-429) but NOT in the Stop hook's file detection logic.

### SUMMARY

**The bug is:** The Stop hook detects file changes via two mechanisms (transcript + git status), but only the transcript extraction is subagent-aware for **token usage calculation**. The **file detection logic** remains agent-only, creating an invisible checkpoint for pure-subagent modifications.

The fix requires extracting subagent file modifications alongside main agent modifications in the Stop hook's `commitWithMetadata` function, using the same pattern already proven in `CalculateTotalTokenUsage`.

> AGENT

Good, I have a solid understanding of the codebase now. Here's the situation:

**The bug**: When subagents (via the `Task` tool) modify files, the Stop hook's `commitWithMetadata` only parses the **main agent transcript** for Write/Edit tool calls. Subagent modifications live in separate `agent-*.jsonl` files that are never scanned during checkpoint detection. Result: `totalChanges = 0` → checkpoint skipped entirely.

**Interesting finding**: The codebase already has the pattern for reading subagent transcripts — `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` in `transcript.go` already iterates over subagent transcripts using `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()`. The file detection logic just doesn't use it.

The issue lists three approaches. Before I propose designs, one clarifying question:

     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"bytes"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"strings"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    17→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    18→
    19→// Type aliases for internal use.
    20→type (
    21→	userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
    22→	assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage
    23→	toolInput        = transcript.ToolInput
    24→)
    25→
    26→// Scanner buffer size for large transcript files (10MB)
    27→const scannerBufferSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024
    28→
    29→// ParseTranscript parses raw JSONL content into transcript lines
    30→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) ([]TranscriptLine, error) {
    31→	var lines []TranscriptLine
    32→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(bytes.NewReader(data))
    33→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, scannerBufferSize), scannerBufferSize)
    34→
    35→	for scanner.Scan() {
    36→		var line TranscriptLine
    37→		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &line); err != nil {
    38→			continue // Skip malformed lines
    39→		}
    40→		lines = append(lines, line)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
    44→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan transcript: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→	return lines, nil
    47→}
    48→
    49→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes
    50→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
    51→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    52→	for _, line := range lines {
    53→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
    54→		if err != nil {
    55→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
    56→		}
    57→		buf.Write(data)
    58→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
    59→	}
    60→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript
    64→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
    65→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    66→	var files []string
    67→
    68→	for _, line := range lines {
    69→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
    70→			continue
    71→		}
    72→
    73→		var msg assistantMessage
    74→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    75→			continue
    76→		}
    77→
    78→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    79→			if block.Type != "tool_use" {
    80→				continue
    81→			}
    82→
    83→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    84→			isModifyTool := false
    85→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    86→				if block.Name == name {
    87→					isModifyTool = true
    88→					break
    89→				}
    90→			}
    91→
    92→			if !isModifyTool {
    93→				continue
    94→			}
    95→
    96→			var input toolInput
    97→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
    98→				continue
    99→			}
   100→
   101→			file := input.FilePath
   102→			if file == "" {
   103→				file = input.NotebookPath
   104→			}
   105→
   106→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   107→				fileSet[file] = true
   108→				files = append(files, file)
   109→			}
   110→		}
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	return files
   114→}
   115→
   116→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript
   117→func ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines []TranscriptLine) string {
   118→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   119→		if lines[i].Type != "user" { //nolint:goconst // already present in codebase
   120→			continue
   121→		}
   122→
   123→		var msg userMessage
   124→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   125→			continue
   126→		}
   127→
   128→		// Handle string content
   129→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   130→			return str
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		// Handle array content (text blocks)
   134→		if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   135→			var texts []string
   136→			for _, item := range arr {
   137→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   138→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   139→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   140→							texts = append(texts, text)
   141→						}
   142→					}
   143→				}
   144→			}
   145→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   146→				return strings.Join(texts, "\n\n")
   147→			}
   148→		}
   149→	}
   150→	return ""
   151→}
   152→
   153→// TruncateAtUUID returns transcript lines up to and including the line with given UUID
   154→func TruncateAtUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, uuid string) []TranscriptLine {
   155→	if uuid == "" {
   156→		return lines
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	for i, line := range lines {
   160→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   161→			return lines[:i+1]
   162→		}
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// UUID not found, return full transcript
   166→	return lines
   167→}
   168→
   169→// toolResultBlock represents a tool_result in a user message
   170→type toolResultBlock struct {
   171→	Type      string `json:"type"`
   172→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   173→}
   174→
   175→// userMessageWithToolResults represents a user message that may contain tool results
   176→type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
   177→	Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
   178→}
   179→
   180→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   181→// for the given tool_use_id
   182→func FindCheckpointUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   183→	for _, line := range lines {
   184→		if line.Type != "user" {
   185→			continue
   186→		}
   187→
   188→		var msg userMessageWithToolResults
   189→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   190→			continue
   191→		}
   192→
   193→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   194→			if block.Type == "tool_result" && block.ToolUseID == toolUseID {
   195→				return line.UUID, true
   196→			}
   197→		}
   198→	}
   199→	return "", false
   200→}
   201→
   202→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript.
   203→// This is specific to Claude/Anthropic's API format where each assistant message
   204→// contains a usage object with input_tokens, output_tokens, and cache tokens.
   205→//
   206→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
   207→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
   208→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcript []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
   209→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
   210→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
   211→
   212→	for _, line := range transcript {
   213→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   214→			continue
   215→		}
   216→
   217→		var msg messageWithUsage
   218→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   219→			continue
   220→		}
   221→
   222→		if msg.ID == "" {
   223→			continue
   224→		}
   225→
   226→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   227→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   228→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   229→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Sum up all unique messages
   234→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   235→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   236→	}
   237→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   238→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   239→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   240→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   241→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	return usage
   245→}
   246→
   247→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript file.
   248→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   249→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   250→	if path == "" {
   251→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(path, startLine)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		return nil, err
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return CalculateTokenUsage(transcript), nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→// parseTranscriptFromLine parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.
   263→func parseTranscriptFromLine(path string, startLine int) ([]TranscriptLine, error) {
   264→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   265→	if err != nil {
   266→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   267→	}
   268→	defer file.Close()
   269→
   270→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   271→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
   272→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, scannerBufferSize), scannerBufferSize)
   273→
   274→	lineNum := 0
   275→	for scanner.Scan() {
   276→		if lineNum < startLine {
   277→			lineNum++
   278→			continue
   279→		}
   280→		lineNum++
   281→
   282→		var line TranscriptLine
   283→		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &line); err != nil {
   284→			continue // Skip malformed lines
   285→		}
   286→		lines = append(lines, line)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
   290→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan transcript: %w", err)
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	return lines, nil
   294→}
   295→
   296→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   297→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   298→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   299→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   300→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   301→
   302→	for _, line := range transcript {
   303→		if line.Type != "user" {
   304→			continue
   305→		}
   306→
   307→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   308→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   309→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   310→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   311→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   312→		}
   313→
   314→		var msg struct {
   315→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   316→		}
   317→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   318→			continue
   319→		}
   320→
   321→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   322→			continue
   323→		}
   324→
   325→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   326→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   327→				continue
   328→			}
   329→
   330→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   331→			var textContent string
   332→
   333→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   334→			var textBlocks []struct {
   335→				Type string `json:"type"`
   336→				Text string `json:"text"`
   337→			}
   338→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   339→				var textContentSb361 strings.Builder
   340→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   341→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   342→						textContentSb361.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   343→					}
   344→				}
   345→				textContent += textContentSb361.String()
   346→			} else {
   347→				// Try as plain string
   348→				var str string
   349→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   350→					textContent = str
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			// Look for agentId in the text
   355→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   356→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   357→			}
   358→		}
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	return agentIDs
   362→}
   363→
   364→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   365→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   366→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   367→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   368→	if idx == -1 {
   369→		return ""
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   373→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   374→	end := start
   375→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   376→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   377→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   378→		end++
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	if end > start {
   382→		return text[start:end]
   383→	}
   384→	return ""
   385→}
   386→
   387→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   388→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   389→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   390→func CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   391→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   392→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   393→	}
   394→
   395→	// Parse transcript ONCE
   396→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   402→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   403→
   404→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   405→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)
   406→
   407→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   408→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   409→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   410→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   411→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   412→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   413→			if err != nil {
   414→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   415→				continue
   416→			}
   417→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   418→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   419→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   420→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   421→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   422→		}
   423→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   424→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   425→		}
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	return mainUsage, nil
   429→}
   430→

     1→// hooks_claudecode_handlers.go contains Claude Code specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"encoding/json"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"

    22→
    23→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    24→type hookInputData struct {
    25→	agent     agent.Agent
    26→	input     *agent.HookInput
    27→	sessionID string
    28→}
    29→
    30→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    31→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    32→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    39→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    45→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    46→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    47→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    48→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    49→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    50→	)
    51→
    52→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    53→	if sessionID == "" {
    54→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    58→	return &hookInputData{
    59→		agent:     ag,
    60→		input:     input,
    61→		sessionID: sessionID,
    62→	}, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    66→func captureInitialState() error {
    67→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    68→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    74→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    75→		return err
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    79→	strat := GetStrategy()
    80→
    81→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (git hooks, gitignore, metadata branch).
    82→	// Done here at turn start so hooks are installed before any mid-turn commits.
    83→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
    84→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    88→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    89→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    90→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    91→		}
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	return nil
    95→}
    96→
    97→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
    98→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    99→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   100→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   106→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   112→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   113→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   114→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   115→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   116→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   117→	)
   118→
   119→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   120→	if sessionID == "" {
   121→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   125→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   130→	// Without this, the function does a lot of work (transcript copy, prompt extraction, etc.)
   131→	// before eventually failing deep in strategy.SaveChanges().
   132→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   133→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   134→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   138→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   139→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   140→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   143→	}
   144→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   149→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   150→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   151→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   152→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   153→
   154→	// Copy transcript
   155→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   156→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   157→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   160→
   161→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   162→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   163→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   169→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   170→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   171→	var transcriptOffset int
   172→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   173→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   174→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   175→	} else {
   176→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   177→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   178→		if loadErr != nil {
   179→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   180→		}
   181→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   182→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   183→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   184→		}
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   188→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   189→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   190→	var totalLines int
   191→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   192→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   193→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   194→		if err != nil {
   195→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   196→		}
   197→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   198→	} else {
   199→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   200→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   201→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   202→		if err != nil {
   203→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   204→		}
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   208→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   209→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   210→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   211→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   212→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   215→
   216→	// Extract summary
   217→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   218→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   219→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   223→
   224→	// Get modified files from transcript
   225→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   226→
   227→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   228→	lastPrompt := ""
   229→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   230→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   231→	}
   232→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   233→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   234→
   235→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   236→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   237→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   238→	if err != nil {
   239→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	if preState != nil {
   243→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   247→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   248→	if err != nil {
   249→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   253→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   254→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   255→	if changes != nil {
   256→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   257→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   261→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   262→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   263→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   264→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   265→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   266→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   267→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   268→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   269→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   270→		}
   271→		return nil
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   275→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   276→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   277→	}
   278→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   279→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   280→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   281→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   282→		}
   283→	}
   284→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   285→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   286→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   287→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   288→		}
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Create context file before saving changes
   292→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   293→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   294→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   295→	}
   296→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   297→
   298→	// Get git author from local/global config
   299→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   300→	if err != nil {
   301→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→
   304→	// Get the configured strategy
   305→	strat := GetStrategy()
   306→
   307→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   308→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   309→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   310→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   314→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   315→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   316→	if preState != nil {
   317→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   318→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   322→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   323→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   324→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   325→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   326→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   327→		if err != nil {
   328→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   329→		} else {
   330→			tokenUsage = usage
   331→		}
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   335→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   336→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   337→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   338→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   339→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   340→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   341→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   342→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   343→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   344→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   345→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   346→		AgentType:                agentType,
   347→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   348→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   349→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   353→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   357→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   358→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   359→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   360→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   361→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   362→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   363→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   364→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   365→		if loadErr != nil {
   366→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   367→		}
   368→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   369→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   370→		if sessionState == nil {
   371→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   372→				SessionID: sessionID,
   373→			}
   374→		}
   375→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   376→		sessionState.StepCount++
   377→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   378→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   379→		} else {
   380→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   381→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   382→		}
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   386→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   387→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   388→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   389→
   390→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   391→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   392→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   393→	}
   394→
   395→	return nil
   396→}
   397→
   398→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   399→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   400→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   401→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   402→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   403→	if err != nil {
   404→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   405→	}
   406→
   407→	// Get agent for logging context
   408→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   409→	if err != nil {
   410→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   411→	}
   412→
   413→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   414→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   415→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   416→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   417→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   418→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   419→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   420→	)
   421→
   422→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   423→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   424→	if !found {
   425→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   426→		return nil
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   430→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   431→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   432→		return nil
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   436→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   437→	if err != nil {
   438→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   439→		return nil
   440→	}
   441→
   442→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   443→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   444→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   445→		return nil
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	// Get git author
   449→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   450→	if err != nil {
   451→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   452→		return nil
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	// Get the active strategy
   456→	strat := GetStrategy()
   457→
   458→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   459→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   460→	if sessionID == "" {
   461→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   465→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   466→
   467→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   468→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   469→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   470→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   471→	if todoContent == "" {
   472→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   473→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   474→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   475→		if todoCount > 0 {
   476→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   477→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   478→		}
   479→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   480→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   484→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   485→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   486→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   490→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   491→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   492→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   493→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   494→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   495→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   496→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   497→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   498→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   499→		IsIncremental:       true,
   500→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   501→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   502→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   503→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   504→		AgentType:           agentType,
   505→	}
   506→
   507→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   508→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   509→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   510→		return nil
   511→	}
   512→
   513→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   514→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   515→	return nil
   516→}
   517→
   518→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   519→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   520→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   521→	if err != nil {
   522→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   523→	}
   524→
   525→	// Get agent for logging context
   526→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   527→	if err != nil {
   528→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   529→	}
   530→
   531→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   532→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   533→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   534→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   535→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   536→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   537→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   538→	)
   539→
   540→	// Log context to stdout
   541→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   542→
   543→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   544→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   545→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   546→	// in file changes.
   547→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   548→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   549→	}
   550→
   551→	return nil
   552→}
   553→
   554→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   555→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   556→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   557→	if err != nil {
   558→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   562→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   563→
   564→	// Get agent for logging context
   565→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   566→	if err != nil {
   567→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	// Log parsed input context
   571→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   572→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   573→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   574→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   575→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   576→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   577→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   578→	)
   579→
   580→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   581→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   582→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   583→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   584→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   585→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   586→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   587→		}
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	// Log context to stdout
   591→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   592→
   593→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   594→	var modifiedFiles []string
   595→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   596→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   597→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   598→		if err != nil {
   599→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   600→		} else {
   601→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   602→		}
   603→	} else {
   604→		// Fall back to main transcript
   605→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   606→		if err != nil {
   607→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   608→		} else {
   609→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   610→		}
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   614→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   615→	if err != nil {
   616→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   617→	}
   618→
   619→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   620→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   621→	if err != nil {
   622→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   623→	}
   624→
   625→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   626→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   627→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   628→	if err != nil {
   629→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	// Filter and normalize paths
   633→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   634→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   635→	if changes != nil {
   636→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   637→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   641→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   642→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   643→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   644→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   645→		return nil
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   649→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   650→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   651→
   652→	// Get git author
   653→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   654→	if err != nil {
   655→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Get the configured strategy
   659→	strat := GetStrategy()
   660→
   661→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   662→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   663→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   664→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   665→	}
   666→
   667→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   668→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   669→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   670→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   671→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   672→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   673→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   674→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   675→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   676→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   677→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   678→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   679→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   680→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   681→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   682→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   683→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   684→		AgentType:              agentType,
   685→	}
   686→
   687→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   688→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   689→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   693→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   694→
   695→	return nil
   696→}
   697→
   698→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   699→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   700→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   701→}
   702→
   703→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   704→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   705→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   706→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   707→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   708→	if err != nil {
   709→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   710→	}
   711→
   712→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   713→	if err != nil {
   714→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   715→	}
   716→
   717→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   718→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   719→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   720→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   721→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   722→	)
   723→
   724→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   725→		return nil // No session to update
   726→	}
   727→
   728→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   729→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   730→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   731→	}
   732→	return nil
   733→}
   734→
   735→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   736→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   737→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   738→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   739→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   740→	if loadErr != nil {
   741→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   742→		return
   743→	}
   744→	if turnState == nil {
   745→		return
   746→	}
   747→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   748→
   749→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   750→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   751→		strat := GetStrategy()
   752→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   753→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   754→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   755→			}
   756→		}
   757→	}
   758→
   759→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   760→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   761→	}
   762→}
   763→
   764→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   765→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   766→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   767→	if err != nil {
   768→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   769→	}
   770→	if state == nil {
   771→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   772→	}
   773→
   774→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   775→
   776→	now := time.Now()
   777→	state.EndedAt = &now
   778→
   779→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   780→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   781→	}
   782→	return nil
   783→}
   784→
   785→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   786→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   787→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   788→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   789→
   790→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   791→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   792→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   793→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   794→//
   795→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   796→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   797→//
   798→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   799→// with whatever data is available.
   800→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   801→	const (
   802→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   803→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   804→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   805→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   806→	)
   807→
   808→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   809→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   810→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   811→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   812→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   813→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   814→			)
   815→			return
   816→		}
   817→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   818→	}
   819→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   820→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   821→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   822→	)
   823→}
   824→
   825→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   826→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   827→// close to hookStartTime.
   828→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   829→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   830→	if err != nil {
   831→		return false
   832→	}
   833→	defer f.Close()
   834→
   835→	// Seek to tail
   836→	info, err := f.Stat()
   837→	if err != nil {
   838→		return false
   839→	}
   840→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   841→	if offset < 0 {
   842→		offset = 0
   843→	}
   844→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   845→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   846→		return false
   847→	}
   848→
   849→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   850→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   851→	for _, line := range lines {
   852→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   853→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   854→			continue
   855→		}
   856→
   857→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   858→		var entry struct {
   859→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   860→		}
   861→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   862→			continue
   863→		}
   864→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   865→		if err != nil {
   866→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   867→			if err != nil {
   868→				continue
   869→			}
   870→		}
   871→
   872→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   873→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   874→			return true
   875→		}
   876→	}
   877→	return false
   878→}
   879→
```

   951→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   952→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   953→//
   954→// Returns true if:
   955→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   956→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   957→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   958→//
   959→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   960→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   961→//
   962→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   963→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   964→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   965→

   969→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   970→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   971→			slog.String("agent_type", string(state.AgentType)),
   972→		)
   973→		return false, nil
   974→	}
   975→

   977→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
   978→	if err != nil {
   979→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Unknown agent type, fail gracefully
   980→	}
   981→
   982→	// Cast to TranscriptAnalyzer
   983→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
   984→	if !ok {
   985→		return false, nil // Agent doesn't support transcript analysis
   986→	}
   987→
   988→	// Get current transcript position
   989→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
   990→	if err != nil {
   991→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error reading transcript, fail gracefully
   992→	}
   993→
   994→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
   995→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
   996→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no new content",
   997→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   998→			slog.Int("current_pos", currentPos),
   999→			slog.Int("start_offset", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
  1000→		)
  1001→		return false, nil // No new content
  1002→	}
  1003→
  1004→	// Transcript has grown - check if there are file modifications in the new portion
  1005→	modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
  1006→	if err != nil {
  1007→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error parsing transcript, fail gracefully

  1010→	// No file modifications means no new content to checkpoint

  1012→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: transcript grew but no file modifications",

  1014→		)
  1015→		return false, nil

  1026→		}

> AGENT

Good question. Let me answer that before continuing with the design.

**Yes, fixing the transcript parsing fixes attribution too.** Here's why:

The `ModifiedFiles` list in `SaveContext` (line 337 of `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`) is entirely transcript-derived. When subagent files are missing from this list:
1. **Missing checkpoints** — `totalChanges == 0` → `SaveChanges` skipped entirely
2. **Misattribution** — even if git status somehow catches the changes (e.g., as untracked files), they end up in `NewFiles`/`DeletedFiles` rather than `ModifiedFiles`, so the checkpoint metadata doesn't properly attribute them to agent tool calls

By parsing subagent transcripts, those file paths flow into `ModifiedFiles` with proper attribution to the agent session.

There's a **second blind spot** though — `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` (line 963 of `manual_commit_hooks.go`). This is called during the `prepare-commit-msg` hook to decide whether to add the `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer to user commits. It calls `analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` on only the main transcript. If the agent delegated all work to subagents, this returns empty → no checkpoint trailer → no metadata linking even when the user commits.

So we need to fix **both** code paths. Let me now propose the approaches.

> TOOL

   377→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   378→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   379→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   380→// Returns:
   381→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   382→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   383→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   384→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   385→	if path == "" {
   386→		return nil, 0, nil
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   390→	if openErr != nil {
   391→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   392→	}
   393→	defer file.Close()
   394→
   395→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   396→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   397→	lineNum := 0
   398→
   399→	for {
   400→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   401→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   402→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   403→		}
   404→
   405→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   406→			lineNum++
   407→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   408→				var line TranscriptLine
   409→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   410→					lines = append(lines, line)
   411→				}
   412→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   413→			}
   414→		}
   415→
   416→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   417→			break
   418→		}
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   422→}
   423→
   424→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   425→
   426→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   427→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   428→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   429→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   430→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   431→	if err != nil {
   432→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→	return chunks, nil
   435→}
   436→
   437→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   438→//
   439→//nolint:unparam // error return is required by interface, kept for consistency
   440→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   441→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   442→}
   443→

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Here's the design:

---

## Design: Fix Subagent File Modifications Invisible to Checkpoint Detection

### Problem Summary

Two code paths only parse the **main agent transcript** for file modifications, missing all subagent work:

| Code Path | Where | Impact |
|-----------|-------|--------|
| `commitWithMetadata()` Stop hook | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:225` | `totalChanges = 0` → checkpoint skipped |
| `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` | `manual_commit_hooks.go:1005` | No checkpoint trailer on user commits |

### Approach: Extend Transcript Parsing to Include Subagent Transcripts

The pattern already exists in `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` (`transcript.go:387-429`): parse main transcript → extract spawned agent IDs via `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()` → read each `agent-<id>.jsonl` → process.

We apply the same pattern to file modification extraction.

### Changes

**1. New function: `ExtractAllModifiedFiles` in `claudecode/transcript.go`**

A new function that combines main + subagent file modifications:

```go
func ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error)
```

Logic:
- Parse main transcript from `startLine`
- Call `ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)` for main agent files
- Call `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(lines)` to find subagent IDs
- For each agent ID, read `<subagentsDir>/agent-<id>.jsonl`, parse, extract modified files
- Deduplicate and return merged list

This mirrors `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` exactly.

**2. Update `commitWithMetadata()` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`**

Replace line 225:
```go
// Before:
modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)

// After: Also extract from subagent transcripts
subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
subagentModifiedFiles := extractSubagentModifiedFiles(transcript, subagentsDir, repoRoot)
modifiedFiles := append(extractModifiedFiles(transcript), subagentModifiedFiles...)
```

The subagent file extraction needs to happen in the CLI layer (not the `claudecode` package) because it uses the CLI's `parseTranscript`/`extractModifiedFiles` wrappers. We'll add a helper `extractSubagentModifiedFiles()` that:
- Calls `claudecode.ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(rawLines)` on the already-parsed transcript (converted to `claudecode.TranscriptLine` format)
- For each subagent, reads and parses their transcript via `parseTranscript()`
- Returns all modified files from subagents

**3. Update `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset` in `claudecode/claude.go`**

This is the `TranscriptAnalyzer` interface method called by `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`. Options:

- **Option A**: Add a new interface method `ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromOffset(path, offset, subagentsDir)` — breaking change to the interface, affects Gemini agent too
- **Option B**: Have the caller (`sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`) do the subagent extraction separately after calling the existing method — no interface change

I recommend **Option B** — it keeps the agent interface unchanged and puts the subagent-awareness in the strategy layer where it belongs (since subagent directory structure is Claude Code-specific anyway).

In `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`:
```go
// After existing ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset call:
modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(...)

// Also check subagent transcripts (Claude Code specific)
if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode {
    subagentFiles := extractSubagentModifiedFilesFromTranscript(state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
    modifiedFiles = append(modifiedFiles, subagentFiles...)
}
```

**4. Tests**

- **Unit test**: `TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_IncludesSubagentFiles` — create a main transcript with Task tool calls and subagent transcript files, verify merged output
- **Unit test for `commitWithMetadata`**: Mock scenario where main transcript has only Task tool_use blocks, subagent transcripts have Write/Edit blocks → verify `totalChanges > 0`
- **Unit test for `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`**: Same scenario → verify returns `true`
- **Integration test**: End-to-end test where subagent-only modifications produce a checkpoint

### What This Does NOT Change

- `DetectFileChanges()` — unchanged, still handles new/deleted untracked files via git status
- The `TranscriptAnalyzer` interface — unchanged
- Gemini agent — unaffected (Gemini doesn't have subagents in the same way)
- `handleClaudeCodePostTask()` — already correctly reads subagent transcripts (line 595-611)

### Files Touched

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `agent/claudecode/transcript.go` | Add `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` |
| `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Update `commitWithMetadata()` to use subagent files |
| `strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` | Update `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` |
| `agent/claudecode/transcript_test.go` | New unit tests |
| `strategy/mid_turn_commit_test.go` | New test for live transcript check |
| `integration_test/` | New integration test |

---

Does this design look right? Any concerns or adjustments before I write it up formally?

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/2/docs/plans/2026-02-13-subagent-checkpoint-detection.md",
  "content": "# Fix Subagent File Modifications Invisible to Checkpoint Detection\n\n**Issue:** [ENT-297](https://linear.app/entirehq/issue/ENT-297/subagent-file-modifications-invisible-to-checkpoint-detection)\n**Date:** 2026-02-13\n\n## Problem\n\n`ExtractModifiedFiles` and related functions only parse the main agent transcript for Write/Edit/NotebookEdit tool calls. When subagents (via the `Task` tool) perform file modifications, those tool calls live in separate `agent-<id>.jsonl` transcript files and are invisible to checkpoint detection.\n\n### Impact\n\n1. **Missing checkpoints**: In the Stop hook, `totalChanges = 0` when only subagents modified files, so `SaveChanges` is skipped entirely.\n2. **Missing checkpoint trailers**: `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` also misses subagent work, so mid-session user commits get no `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer.\n3. **Misattribution**: Subagent file changes appear as \"human\" edits since they're not in the transcript-derived `ModifiedFiles` list.\n\n### Affected Code Paths\n\n| Code Path | Location | Impact |\n|-----------|----------|--------|\n| `commitWithMetadata()` | `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:225` | `totalChanges = 0`, checkpoint skipped |\n| `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` | `manual_commit_hooks.go:1005` | No checkpoint trailer on user commits |\n\n## Approach: Extend Transcript Parsing to Include Subagent Transcripts\n\nThe pattern already exists in `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` (`transcript.go:387-429`): parse main transcript, extract spawned agent IDs via `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()`, read each `agent-<id>.jsonl`, process. We apply the same pattern to file modification extraction.\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. New function in `claudecode/transcript.go`\n\n`ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, startLine, subagentsDir)` combines main + subagent file modifications:\n\n- Parse main transcript from `startLine`\n- Call `ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)` for main agent files\n- Call `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(lines)` to find subagent IDs\n- For each agent ID, read and parse `<subagentsDir>/agent-<id>.jsonl`, extract modified files\n- Deduplicate and return merged list\n\n### 2. Update `commitWithMetadata()` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`\n\nAdd a CLI-layer helper `extractSubagentModifiedFiles(transcript, subagentsDir, repoRoot)` that:\n\n- Calls `claudecode.ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()` on the parsed transcript\n- For each subagent, reads and parses their transcript\n- Returns all modified files from subagents\n\nUpdate line 225 to merge main + subagent modified files.\n\n### 3. Update `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` in `manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\nAfter the existing `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset` call, also check subagent transcripts when `AgentType == ClaudeCode`. No interface change needed — subagent-awareness stays in the strategy layer.\n\n### 4. Tests\n\n- Unit test for `ExtractAllModifiedFiles` with subagent transcripts\n- Unit test for `commitWithMetadata` where only subagents modify files\n- Unit test for `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` with subagent-only changes\n- Integration test for end-to-end subagent checkpoint creation\n\n## What This Does NOT Change\n\n- `DetectFileChanges()` — unchanged, still handles new/deleted untracked files via git status\n- `TranscriptAnalyzer` interface — unchanged\n- Gemini agent — unaffected\n- `handleClaudeCodePostTask()` — already correctly reads subagent transcripts\n\n## Files Touched\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `agent/claudecode/transcript.go` | Add `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` |\n| `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Update `commitWithMetadata()` to merge subagent files |\n| `strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` | Update `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` |\n| `agent/claudecode/transcript_test.go` | New unit tests |\n| `strategy/mid_turn_commit_test.go` | New test for live transcript check |\n| `integration_test/` | New integration test |\n"
}
```
