   324→// ClearSessionState removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   325→// This simulates what happens when a user commits their changes (session is "completed").
   326→// Used in tests to allow sequential sessions to run without triggering concurrent session warnings.
   327→func (env *TestEnv) ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   328→	env.T.Helper()
   329→
   330→	// Session state is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
   331→	stateFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions", sessionID+".json")
   332→
   333→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   334→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   335→	}
   336→	return nil
   337→}

   339→// HookOutput contains the result of running a hook.
   340→type HookOutput struct {
   341→	Stdout []byte
   342→	Stderr []byte
   343→	Err    error
   344→}
   345→
   346→// runHookWithOutput runs a hook and returns both stdout and stderr separately.
   347→func (r *HookRunner) runHookWithOutput(hookName string, inputJSON []byte) HookOutput {
   348→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", hookName)
   349→	cmd.Dir = r.RepoDir
   350→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   351→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   352→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+r.ClaudeProjectDir,
   353→	)
   354→
   355→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   356→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   357→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   358→
   359→	err := cmd.Run()
   360→	return HookOutput{
   361→		Stdout: stdout.Bytes(),
   362→		Stderr: stderr.Bytes(),
   363→		Err:    err,
   364→	}
   365→}

   415→// GetSessionState reads and returns the session state for the given session ID.
   416→func (env *TestEnv) GetSessionState(sessionID string) (*strategy.SessionState, error) {
   417→	env.T.Helper()
   418→
   419→	stateFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions", sessionID+".json")
   420→
   421→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile)
   422→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   423→		return nil, nil
   424→	}
   425→	if err != nil {
   426→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	var state strategy.SessionState
   430→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   431→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session state: %w", err)
   432→	}
   433→	return &state, nil
   434→}

   584→// GeminiSession represents a simulated Gemini CLI session.
   585→type GeminiSession struct {
   586→	ID             string // Raw model session ID (e.g., "gemini-session-1")
   587→	TranscriptPath string
   588→	env            *TestEnv
   589→}
   590→
   591→// NewGeminiSession creates a new simulated Gemini session.
   592→func (env *TestEnv) NewGeminiSession() *GeminiSession {
   593→	env.T.Helper()
   594→
   595→	env.SessionCounter++
   596→	sessionID := fmt.Sprintf("gemini-session-%d", env.SessionCounter)
   597→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp", sessionID+".json")
   598→
   599→	return &GeminiSession{
   600→		ID:             sessionID,
   601→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   602→		env:            env,
   603→	}
   604→}
   605→
   606→// CreateGeminiTranscript creates a Gemini JSON transcript file for the session.
   607→func (s *GeminiSession) CreateGeminiTranscript(prompt string, changes []FileChange) string {
   608→	// Build Gemini-format transcript (JSON, not JSONL)
   609→	messages := []map[string]interface{}{
   610→		{
   611→			"type":    "user",
   612→			"content": prompt,
   613→		},
   614→		{
   615→			"type":    "assistant",
   616→			"content": "I'll help you with that.",
   617→		},
   618→	}
   619→
   620→	for _, change := range changes {
   621→		messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{
   622→			"type": "tool_use",
   623→			"name": "write_file",
   624→			"input": map[string]string{
   625→				"path":    change.Path,
   626→				"content": change.Content,
   627→			},
   628→		})
   629→		messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{
   630→			"type":   "tool_result",
   631→			"output": "File written successfully",
   632→		})
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{
   636→		"type":    "assistant",
   637→		"content": "Done!",
   638→	})
   639→
   640→	transcript := map[string]interface{}{
   641→		"sessionId": s.ID,
   642→		"messages":  messages,
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// Ensure directory exists
   646→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(s.TranscriptPath), 0o755); err != nil {
   647→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to create transcript dir: %v", err)
   648→	}
   649→
   650→	// Write transcript
   651→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(transcript, "", "  ")
   652→	if err != nil {
   653→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to marshal transcript: %v", err)
   654→	}
   655→	if err := os.WriteFile(s.TranscriptPath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   656→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	return s.TranscriptPath
   660→}
   661→

   664→	env.T.Helper()

   666→	return runner.SimulateGeminiBeforeAgent(sessionID)

     1→//go:build integration

     3→package integration
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"time"

    13→
    14→// TranscriptBuilder builds realistic JSONL transcripts for testing.
    15→type TranscriptBuilder struct {
    16→	messages       []map[string]interface{}
    17→	toolUseCounter int
    18→}
    19→
    20→// NewTranscriptBuilder creates a new transcript builder.
    21→func NewTranscriptBuilder() *TranscriptBuilder {
    22→	return &TranscriptBuilder{
    23→		messages: make([]map[string]interface{}, 0),
    24→	}
    25→}
    26→
    27→// AddUserMessage adds a user message with string content.
    28→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddUserMessage(content string) *TranscriptBuilder {
    29→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    30→		"uuid":      fmt.Sprintf("user-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    31→		"type":      "user",
    32→		"message":   map[string]interface{}{"content": content},
    33→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    34→	})
    35→	return b
    36→}
    37→
    38→// AddAssistantMessage adds an assistant message with text content.
    39→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddAssistantMessage(content string) *TranscriptBuilder {
    40→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    41→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("asst-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    42→		"type": "assistant",
    43→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
    44→			"content": []map[string]interface{}{
    45→				{"type": "text", "text": content},
    46→			},
    47→		},
    48→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    49→	})
    50→	return b
    51→}
    52→
    53→// AddToolUse adds a tool use (Write/Edit) to the transcript.
    54→// Returns the tool use ID for use with AddToolResult.
    55→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddToolUse(toolName, filePath, content string) string {
    56→	b.toolUseCounter++
    57→	toolUseID := fmt.Sprintf("toolu_%d", b.toolUseCounter)
    58→
    59→	toolUse := map[string]interface{}{
    60→		"type": "tool_use",
    61→		"id":   toolUseID,
    62→		"name": toolName,
    63→		"input": map[string]interface{}{
    64→			"file_path": filePath,
    65→			"content":   content,
    66→		},
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    70→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("asst-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    71→		"type": "assistant",
    72→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
    73→			"content": []interface{}{toolUse},
    74→		},
    75→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    76→	})
    77→
    78→	return toolUseID
    79→}
    80→
    81→// AddToolResult adds a tool result for a previous tool use.
    82→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddToolResult(toolUseID string) *TranscriptBuilder {
    83→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    84→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("user-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    85→		"type": "user",
    86→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
    87→			"content": []map[string]interface{}{
    88→				{
    89→					"type":        "tool_result",
    90→					"tool_use_id": toolUseID,
    91→					"content":     "Success",
    92→				},
    93→			},
    94→		},
    95→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    96→	})
    97→	return b
    98→}
    99→
   100→// AddTaskToolUse adds a Task tool invocation (for subagent calls).
   101→// Returns the tool use ID for use with AddTaskToolResult.
   102→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddTaskToolUse(toolUseID, prompt string) string {
   103→	if toolUseID == "" {
   104→		b.toolUseCounter++
   105→		toolUseID = fmt.Sprintf("toolu_%d", b.toolUseCounter)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	toolUse := map[string]interface{}{
   109→		"type": "tool_use",
   110→		"id":   toolUseID,
   111→		"name": "Task",
   112→		"input": map[string]interface{}{
   113→			"prompt":        prompt,
   114→			"subagent_type": "general-purpose",
   115→		},
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   119→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("asst-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
   120→		"type": "assistant",
   121→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
   122→			"content": []interface{}{toolUse},
   123→		},
   124→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   125→	})
   126→
   127→	return toolUseID
   128→}
   129→
   130→// AddTaskToolResult adds a tool result for a Task tool invocation.
   131→// The UUID of this message is used as the checkpoint UUID for rewind.
   132→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddTaskToolResult(toolUseID, agentID string) string {
   133→	uuid := fmt.Sprintf("user-%d", len(b.messages)+1)
   134→
   135→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   136→		"uuid": uuid,
   137→		"type": "user",
   138→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
   139→			"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   140→				{
   141→					"type":        "tool_result",
   142→					"tool_use_id": toolUseID,
   143→					"content":     "Task completed by agent " + agentID,
   144→				},
   145→			},
   146→		},
   147→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   148→	})
   149→
   150→	return uuid
   151→}
   152→
   153→// WriteToFile writes the transcript to a JSONL file.
   154→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) WriteToFile(path string) error {
   155→	// Ensure directory exists
   156→	dir := filepath.Dir(path)
   157→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	file, err := os.Create(path)
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create file: %w", err)
   164→	}
   165→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
   166→
   167→	encoder := json.NewEncoder(file)
   168→	for _, msg := range b.messages {
   169→		if err := encoder.Encode(msg); err != nil {
   170→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode message: %w", err)
   171→		}
   172→	}
   173→	return nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// String returns the transcript as a JSONL string.
   177→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) String() string {
   178→	var result string
   179→	var resultSb176 strings.Builder
   180→	for _, msg := range b.messages {
   181→		data, _ := json.Marshal(msg)
   182→		resultSb176.WriteString(string(data) + "\n")
   183→	}
   184→	result += resultSb176.String()
   185→	return result
   186→}
   187→
   188→// LastUUID returns the UUID of the last message added.
   189→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) LastUUID() string {
   190→	if len(b.messages) == 0 {
   191→		return ""
   192→	}
   193→	return b.messages[len(b.messages)-1]["uuid"].(string)
   194→}
   195→

     1→//go:build integration

     3→package integration

     5→import (

    10→	"testing"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"

    19→
    20→// TestSubagentCheckpoints_FullFlow tests the complete subagent checkpoint flow:
    21→// PreTask -> PostTodo (multiple times with file changes) -> PostTask
    22→//
    23→// This verifies:
    24→// 1. Incremental checkpoints are created as commits during subagent execution
    25→// 2. Only PostTodo calls with file changes create commits
    26→// 3. PostTask creates the final task checkpoint commit
    27→func TestSubagentCheckpoints_FullFlow(t *testing.T) {
    28→	t.Parallel()
    29→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    30→		// Create a session
    31→		session := env.NewSession()
    32→
    33→		// Create transcript (needed by hooks)
    34→		session.CreateTranscript("Implement feature X", []FileChange{
    35→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main"},
    36→		})
    37→
    38→		// Simulate user prompt submit first (captures pre-prompt state)
    39→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
    40→		if err != nil {
    41→			t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
    42→		}
    43→
    44→		// Task tool use ID (simulates Claude's Task tool invocation)
    45→		taskToolUseID := "toolu_01TaskABC123"
    46→
    47→		// Step 1: PreTask - creates pre-task file
    48→		err = env.SimulatePreTask(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, taskToolUseID)
    49→		if err != nil {
    50→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePreTask failed: %v", err)
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		// Verify pre-task file was created
    54→		preTaskFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp", "pre-task-"+taskToolUseID+".json")
    55→		if _, err := os.Stat(preTaskFile); os.IsNotExist(err) {
    56→			t.Error("pre-task file should exist after SimulatePreTask")
    57→		}
    58→
    59→		// Step 2: PostTodo - simulate TodoWrite calls with file changes between them
    60→		// Note: Only PostTodo calls that detect file changes will create incremental commits
    61→
    62→		// First TodoWrite - no file changes, should be skipped
    63→		err = env.SimulatePostTodo(PostTodoInput{
    64→			SessionID:      session.ID,
    65→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
    66→			ToolUseID:      "toolu_01TodoWrite001",
    67→			Todos: []Todo{
    68→				{Content: "Create feature file", Status: "in_progress", ActiveForm: "Creating feature file"},
    69→				{Content: "Write tests", Status: "pending", ActiveForm: "Writing tests"},
    70→			},
    71→		})
    72→		if err != nil {
    73→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTodo failed for first todo: %v", err)
    74→		}
    75→
    76→		// Create a file change
    77→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n")
    78→
    79→		// Second TodoWrite - should create incremental checkpoint (has file changes)
    80→		err = env.SimulatePostTodo(PostTodoInput{
    81→			SessionID:      session.ID,
    82→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
    83→			ToolUseID:      "toolu_01TodoWrite002",
    84→			Todos: []Todo{
    85→				{Content: "Create feature file", Status: "completed", ActiveForm: "Creating feature file"},
    86→				{Content: "Write tests", Status: "in_progress", ActiveForm: "Writing tests"},
    87→			},
    88→		})
    89→		if err != nil {
    90→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTodo failed for second todo: %v", err)
    91→		}
    92→
    93→		// Create another file change
    94→		env.WriteFile("feature_test.go", "package main\n\nimport \"testing\"\n\nfunc TestFeature(t *testing.T) {}\n")
    95→
    96→		// Third TodoWrite - should create another incremental checkpoint
    97→		err = env.SimulatePostTodo(PostTodoInput{
    98→			SessionID:      session.ID,
    99→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
   100→			ToolUseID:      "toolu_01TodoWrite003",
   101→			Todos: []Todo{
   102→				{Content: "Create feature file", Status: "completed", ActiveForm: "Creating feature file"},
   103→				{Content: "Write tests", Status: "completed", ActiveForm: "Writing tests"},
   104→			},
   105→		})
   106→		if err != nil {
   107→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTodo failed for third todo: %v", err)
   108→		}
   109→
   110→		// Step 3: PostTask - creates final task checkpoint
   111→		err = env.SimulatePostTask(PostTaskInput{
   112→			SessionID:      session.ID,
   113→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
   114→			ToolUseID:      taskToolUseID,
   115→			AgentID:        "agent-123",
   116→		})
   117→		if err != nil {
   118→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTask failed: %v", err)
   119→		}
   120→
   121→		// Verify pre-task file is cleaned up
   122→		if _, err := os.Stat(preTaskFile); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   123→			t.Error("Pre-task file should be removed after PostTask")
   124→		}
   125→
   126→		// Verify checkpoints are stored in final location (strategy-specific)
   127→		verifyCheckpointStorage(t, env, strategyName, session.ID, taskToolUseID)
   128→	})
   129→}
   130→
   131→// TestSubagentCheckpoints_NoFileChanges tests that PostTodo is skipped when no file changes
   132→func TestSubagentCheckpoints_NoFileChanges(t *testing.T) {
   133→	t.Parallel()
   134→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   135→		// Create a session
   136→		session := env.NewSession()
   137→
   138→		// Create transcript
   139→		session.CreateTranscript("Quick task", []FileChange{})
   140→
   141→		// Simulate user prompt submit
   142→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   143→		if err != nil {
   144→			t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   145→		}
   146→
   147→		// Create pre-task file to simulate subagent context
   148→		taskToolUseID := "toolu_01TaskNoChanges"
   149→		err = env.SimulatePreTask(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, taskToolUseID)
   150→		if err != nil {
   151→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePreTask failed: %v", err)
   152→		}
   153→
   154→		// Get git log before PostTodo
   155→		beforeCommits := env.GetGitLog()
   156→
   157→		// Call PostTodo WITHOUT making any file changes
   158→		err = env.SimulatePostTodo(PostTodoInput{
   159→			SessionID:      session.ID,
   160→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
   161→			ToolUseID:      "toolu_01TodoWriteNoChange",
   162→			Todos: []Todo{
   163→				{Content: "Some task", Status: "pending", ActiveForm: "Doing task"},
   164→			},
   165→		})
   166→		if err != nil {
   167→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTodo should not fail: %v", err)
   168→		}
   169→
   170→		// Get git log after PostTodo
   171→		afterCommits := env.GetGitLog()
   172→
   173→		// Verify no new commits were created
   174→		if len(afterCommits) != len(beforeCommits) {
   175→			t.Errorf("Expected no new commits when no file changes, before=%d after=%d", len(beforeCommits), len(afterCommits))
   176→		}
   177→	})
   178→}
   179→
   180→// TestSubagentCheckpoints_PostTaskNoFileChanges tests that PostTask is skipped when no file changes
   181→// and the pre-task state is still cleaned up.
   182→func TestSubagentCheckpoints_PostTaskNoFileChanges(t *testing.T) {
   183→	t.Parallel()
   184→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   185→		// Create a session
   186→		session := env.NewSession()
   187→
   188→		// Create transcript (no file changes in transcript either)
   189→		session.CreateTranscript("Quick task with no file changes", []FileChange{})
   190→
   191→		// Simulate user prompt submit
   192→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   193→		if err != nil {
   194→			t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   195→		}
   196→
   197→		// Create pre-task file to simulate subagent context
   198→		taskToolUseID := "toolu_01TaskNoFileChanges"
   199→		err = env.SimulatePreTask(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, taskToolUseID)
   200→		if err != nil {
   201→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePreTask failed: %v", err)
   202→		}
   203→
   204→		// Verify pre-task file was created
   205→		preTaskFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp", "pre-task-"+taskToolUseID+".json")
   206→		if _, err := os.Stat(preTaskFile); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   207→			t.Fatal("pre-task file should exist after SimulatePreTask")
   208→		}
   209→
   210→		// Get git log before PostTask
   211→		beforeCommits := env.GetGitLog()
   212→
   213→		// Call PostTask WITHOUT making any file changes
   214→		err = env.SimulatePostTask(PostTaskInput{
   215→			SessionID:      session.ID,
   216→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
   217→			ToolUseID:      taskToolUseID,
   218→			AgentID:        "agent-no-changes",
   219→		})
   220→		if err != nil {
   221→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTask should not fail: %v", err)
   222→		}
   223→
   224→		// Get git log after PostTask
   225→		afterCommits := env.GetGitLog()
   226→
   227→		// Verify no new commits were created on the main branch
   228→		if len(afterCommits) != len(beforeCommits) {
   229→			t.Errorf("Expected no new commits when no file changes, before=%d after=%d", len(beforeCommits), len(afterCommits))
   230→		}
   231→
   232→		// Verify pre-task file is cleaned up even though no checkpoint was created
   233→		if _, err := os.Stat(preTaskFile); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   234→			t.Error("Pre-task file should be removed after PostTask even with no file changes")
   235→		}
   236→	})
   237→}
   238→
   239→// TestSubagentCheckpoints_NoPreTaskFile tests that PostTodo is a no-op
   240→// when there's no active pre-task file (main agent context).
   241→func TestSubagentCheckpoints_NoPreTaskFile(t *testing.T) {
   242→	t.Parallel()
   243→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   244→		// Create a session
   245→		session := env.NewSession()
   246→
   247→		// Create transcript
   248→		session.CreateTranscript("Quick task", []FileChange{})
   249→
   250→		// Simulate user prompt submit
   251→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   252→		if err != nil {
   253→			t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   254→		}
   255→
   256→		// Create a file change so that PostTodo would trigger if in subagent context
   257→		env.WriteFile("test.txt", "content")
   258→
   259→		// Get git log before PostTodo
   260→		beforeCommits := env.GetGitLog()
   261→
   262→		// Call PostTodo WITHOUT calling PreTask first
   263→		// This simulates a TodoWrite from the main agent (not a subagent)
   264→		err = env.SimulatePostTodo(PostTodoInput{
   265→			SessionID:      session.ID,
   266→			TranscriptPath: session.TranscriptPath,
   267→			ToolUseID:      "toolu_01MainAgentTodo",
   268→			Todos: []Todo{
   269→				{Content: "Some task", Status: "pending", ActiveForm: "Doing task"},
   270→			},
   271→		})
   272→		if err != nil {
   273→			t.Fatalf("SimulatePostTodo should not fail: %v", err)
   274→		}
   275→
   276→		// Get git log after PostTodo
   277→		afterCommits := env.GetGitLog()
   278→
   279→		// Verify no new commits were created (not in subagent context)
   280→		if len(afterCommits) != len(beforeCommits) {
   281→			t.Errorf("Expected no new commits when not in subagent context, before=%d after=%d", len(beforeCommits), len(afterCommits))
   282→		}
   283→	})
   284→}
   285→
   286→// verifyCheckpointStorage verifies that checkpoints are stored in the correct
   287→// location based on the strategy type.
   288→// Note: Incremental checkpoints are stored in separate commits during task execution,
   289→// while the final checkpoint.json is created at PostTask time.
   290→func verifyCheckpointStorage(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName, sessionID, taskToolUseID string) {
   291→	t.Helper()
   292→
   293→	switch strategyName {
   294→	case strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit:
   295→		// Shadow strategy stores checkpoints in git tree on shadow branch (entire/<head-hash>)
   296→		// We need to verify that checkpoint data exists in the shadow branch tree
   297→		verifyShadowCheckpointStorage(t, env, sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   298→
   299→	case strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit:
   300→		// Dual strategy stores metadata on orphan entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   301→		// Verify that commits were created (incremental + final)
   302→		t.Logf("Note: auto-commit strategy stores checkpoints in entire/checkpoints/v1 branch")
   303→	}
   304→}
   305→
   306→// verifyShadowCheckpointStorage verifies that checkpoints are stored in the shadow branch git tree.
   307→func verifyShadowCheckpointStorage(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, sessionID, taskToolUseID string) {
   308→	t.Helper()
   309→
   310→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   311→	if err != nil {
   312→		t.Fatalf("failed to open repo: %v", err)
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Get shadow branch name using worktree-specific naming
   316→	shadowBranchName := env.GetShadowBranchName()
   317→
   318→	// Get shadow branch reference
   319→	shadowRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName), true)
   320→	if err != nil {
   321→		t.Fatalf("shadow branch %s not found: %v", shadowBranchName, err)
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	// Get the commit and tree from shadow branch
   325→	shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   326→	if err != nil {
   327→		t.Fatalf("failed to get shadow commit: %v", err)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	shadowTree, err := shadowCommit.Tree()
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		t.Fatalf("failed to get shadow tree: %v", err)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	// Look for task metadata in the tree
   336→	// Path format: .entire/metadata/<session-id>/tasks/<task-id>/
   337→	taskMetadataPrefix := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID + "/tasks/" + taskToolUseID + "/"
   338→	checkpointsPrefix := taskMetadataPrefix + "checkpoints/"
   339→
   340→	foundCheckpoint := false
   341→	foundCheckpointFiles := 0
   342→
   343→	err = shadowTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   344→		// Check for checkpoint file (final checkpoint)
   345→		if f.Name == taskMetadataPrefix+paths.CheckpointFileName {
   346→			foundCheckpoint = true
   347→			// Verify content is valid JSON
   348→			content, readErr := f.Contents()
   349→			if readErr != nil {
   350→				t.Errorf("failed to read %s: %v", paths.CheckpointFileName, readErr)
   351→				return nil
   352→			}
   353→			var cp strategy.TaskCheckpoint
   354→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &cp); jsonErr != nil {
   355→				t.Errorf("%s is invalid JSON: %v", paths.CheckpointFileName, jsonErr)
   356→			}
   357→		}
   358→
   359→		// Check for incremental checkpoints in checkpoints/ directory
   360→		if strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, checkpointsPrefix) && strings.HasSuffix(f.Name, ".json") {
   361→			foundCheckpointFiles++
   362→			// Verify content is valid checkpoint JSON
   363→			content, readErr := f.Contents()
   364→			if readErr != nil {
   365→				t.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint file %s: %v", f.Name, readErr)
   366→				return nil
   367→			}
   368→			var cp strategy.SubagentCheckpoint
   369→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &cp); jsonErr != nil {
   370→				t.Errorf("checkpoint file %s is invalid JSON: %v", f.Name, jsonErr)
   371→			}
   372→			// Verify required fields
   373→			if cp.Type == "" {
   374→				t.Errorf("checkpoint file %s missing type field", f.Name)
   375→			}
   376→			if cp.ToolUseID == "" {
   377→				t.Errorf("checkpoint file %s missing tool_use_id field", f.Name)
   378→			}
   379→		}
   380→
   381→		return nil
   382→	})
   383→	if err != nil {
   384→		t.Fatalf("failed to iterate shadow tree: %v", err)
   385→	}
   386→
   387→	if !foundCheckpoint {
   388→		t.Errorf("%s not found in shadow branch tree at %s", paths.CheckpointFileName, taskMetadataPrefix+paths.CheckpointFileName)
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	if foundCheckpointFiles == 0 {
   392→		t.Logf("Note: no incremental checkpoint files found in %s - they may be in earlier commits", checkpointsPrefix)
   393→	} else {
   394→		t.Logf("Found %d incremental checkpoint files in shadow branch tree", foundCheckpointFiles)
   395→	}
   396→}
   397→

   128→// TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsOverwrittenHooks verifies that EnsureSetup is called
   129→// during user-prompt-submit (start of turn) and reinstalls hooks that were overwritten
   130→// by third-party tools like lefthook. This ensures hooks are in place before any
   131→// mid-turn commits the agent might make.
   132→func TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsOverwrittenHooks(t *testing.T) {
   133→	t.Parallel()
   134→	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   135→		hooksDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "hooks")
   136→		hookNames := strategy.ManagedGitHookNames()
   137→
   138→		// Step 1: First user-prompt-submit installs hooks via EnsureSetup
   139→		session := env.NewSession()
   140→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   141→		if err != nil {
   142→			t.Fatalf("First SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   143→		}
   144→
   145→		// Verify hooks are now installed
   146→		if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalledInDir(env.RepoDir) {
   147→			t.Fatal("hooks should be installed after first SimulateUserPromptSubmit")
   148→		}
   149→
   150→		// Step 2: Overwrite hooks with third-party content (simulating lefthook, husky, etc.)
   151→		for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   152→			hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hookName)
   153→			thirdPartyContent := "#!/bin/sh\n# Third-party hook manager\necho 'Running third-party hook'\n"
   154→			if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(thirdPartyContent), 0o755); err != nil {
   155→				t.Fatalf("failed to overwrite hook %s: %v", hookName, err)
   156→			}
   157→		}
   158→
   159→		// Step 3: Verify hooks are no longer Entire hooks
   160→		if strategy.IsGitHookInstalledInDir(env.RepoDir) {
   161→			t.Fatal("hooks should NOT be detected as Entire hooks after overwrite")
   162→		}
   163→
   164→		// Step 4: Second user-prompt-submit should reinstall hooks
   165→		err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   166→		if err != nil {
   167→			t.Fatalf("Second SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   168→		}
   169→
   170→		// Step 5: Verify hooks are reinstalled
   171→		if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalledInDir(env.RepoDir) {
   172→			t.Error("hooks should be reinstalled after second SimulateUserPromptSubmit")
   173→		}
   174→
   175→		// Step 6: Verify the hooks chain to original hooks (backup should exist)
   176→		for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   177→			backupPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hookName+".pre-entire")
   178→			if _, err := os.Stat(backupPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   179→				t.Errorf("backup hook %s.pre-entire should exist", hookName)
   180→			}
   181→		}
   182→	})
   183→}
   184→
   185→// TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsDeletedHooks verifies that EnsureSetup reinstalls
   186→// hooks that were completely deleted by third-party tools.
   187→func TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsDeletedHooks(t *testing.T) {
   188→	t.Parallel()
   189→	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   190→		hooksDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "hooks")

{
  "description": "Implement integration test for subagent-only checkpoint",
  "subagent_type": "dev",
  "prompt": "\n## Task: Write Integration Test for Subagent-Only File Modification Checkpoint\n\n### Context\n\nWe fixed a bug (ENT-297) where subagent file modifications were invisible to checkpoint detection. When only subagents (spawned via the Task tool) modified files, checkpoints were skipped because `totalChanges = 0`. We've already fixed the code in three prior commits. Now we need an integration test proving the fix works end-to-end.\n\n### What You Need to Know\n\n**The bug:** The Stop hook in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` extracts modified files from the transcript to decide whether to create a checkpoint. Previously, it only looked at the main agent's transcript. When subagents (spawned via the Task tool) performed all file modifications, those tool calls lived in separate `agent-<id>.jsonl` files and were invisible, causing `totalChanges = 0` and the checkpoint to be skipped.\n\n**The fix:** We added `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` which also reads subagent transcript files. The Stop hook now uses this function.\n\n**Subagent transcript layout:**\n- Main transcript: `<some-path>/transcript.jsonl`  \n- Subagent transcripts: `<some-path>/<sessionID>/subagents/agent-<agentID>.jsonl`\n\n### Your Task\n\nWrite an integration test in `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks_test.go` that:\n\n1. Creates a session and calls SimulateUserPromptSubmit\n2. Creates a file on disk (simulating what a subagent would write)\n3. Creates a main transcript that only has a Task tool call (NO Write/Edit calls)\n4. Creates a subagent transcript file (in the correct directory structure) that has Write tool calls referencing the created file\n5. Calls SimulateStop with the transcript path\n6. Verifies the stop hook does NOT error\n7. For auto-commit strategy: verifies a checkpoint was created (new commit on active branch)\n8. For manual-commit strategy: verifies the shadow branch has checkpoint data\n\n### Key Integration Test Patterns\n\nThe test should follow the pattern from `TestHookRunner_SimulateStop` in `hooks_test.go`:\n\n```go\nfunc TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {\n    t.Parallel()\n    RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {\n        // ... test body\n    })\n}\n```\n\n**Important:** The test MUST use `RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv` (not feature branch) since stop hook tests work on any branch.\n\n### Transcript Format\n\nThe `TranscriptBuilder` in `integration_test/transcript.go` has methods:\n- `AddTaskToolUse(toolUseID, prompt)` - adds a Task tool invocation\n- `AddTaskToolResult(toolUseID, agentID)` - adds a Task tool result with agent ID\n- `AddToolUse(toolName, filePath, content)` - adds a Write/Edit tool use\n- `AddToolResult(toolUseID)` - adds a tool result\n- `WriteToFile(path)` - writes the transcript JSONL\n\nThe subagent transcript files need to be in the Claude Code format. They are separate JSONL files that follow the same format as the main transcript. The important thing is the path: `<transcriptDir>/<sessionID>/subagents/agent-<agentID>.jsonl`.\n\nThe `input.SessionID` in the Stop hook is the model session ID, which is `session.ID` in the test framework.\n\n### How SimulateStop Works\n\nWhen `SimulateStop` is called, it runs `entire hooks claude-code stop` with the session ID and transcript path as JSON input. The Stop hook:\n1. Reads the transcript\n2. Calls `ExtractAllModifiedFiles` (our fix!) to get modified files from main + subagent transcripts\n3. Detects file changes on disk (via `git status`)\n4. If `totalChanges > 0`, creates a checkpoint\n\nThe `ClaudeProjectDir` in the test env is used for agent session data. The transcript path can be anywhere (the test framework puts it in `.entire/tmp/`).\n\n### Subagent Transcript File Format\n\nA subagent transcript is just a JSONL file with the same message format. For the test, you can use a separate `TranscriptBuilder` to create it. The subagent transcript should have Write tool calls with `file_path` pointing to the actual file path in the test repo.\n\nExample subagent transcript entry (JSONL line):\n```json\n{\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"type\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"tool_use\",\"id\":\"toolu_1\",\"name\":\"Write\",\"input\":{\"file_path\":\"/absolute/path/to/file.go\",\"content\":\"package main\"}}]},\"timestamp\":\"2026-01-01T00:00:00Z\"}\n```\n\nNote the tool name should be `Write` (or `mcp__acp__Write` which the existing code also handles) - check what `extractModifiedFiles` actually looks for.\n\n### What extractModifiedFiles Looks For\n\nThe `ExtractModifiedFiles` function in `transcript.go` looks for tool_use blocks with names:\n- `Write` \n- `Edit`\n- `NotebookEdit`\n- `mcp__acp__Write`\n- `mcp__acp__Edit`\n\nIt extracts the `file_path` from the `input` field.\n\n### Verification\n\n**For auto-commit strategy:** A new commit should be created on the active branch. Check with `env.GetGitLog()` comparing before/after commit count, or check HEAD commit message.\n\n**For manual-commit strategy:** A checkpoint should be stored on the shadow branch. Check `env.BranchExists(env.GetShadowBranchName())` to verify the shadow branch was created, and/or check the session state.\n\n**For both strategies:** The SimulateStop call should NOT return an error.\n\n### File Location\n\nAdd the test to: `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks_test.go`\n\n### Build Tag\n\nThe file already has `//go:build integration` at the top.\n\n### Running Tests\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/2 && go test -tags integration ./cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/ -run TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges -v -count=1\n```\n\nThen verify full integration test suite passes:\n```bash\ncd /Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/2 && mise run test:integration\n```\n\nAnd then lint:\n```bash\ncd /Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/2 && mise run fmt && mise run lint\n```\n\n### Commit\n\nAfter tests pass:\n```bash\ngit add cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks_test.go\ngit commit -m \"test: add integration test for subagent-only checkpoint creation (ENT-297)\"\n```\n\nUse the HEREDOC format for the commit message.\n"
}

Here is a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary

### What was implemented

An integration test `TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges` was added to `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/2/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks_test.go`. This test is a regression test for ENT-297 that verifies the Stop hook correctly creates a checkpoint when **only** subagents (spawned via the Task tool) modify files.

### How the test works

1. **Creates a session** and calls `SimulateUserPromptSubmit` to capture pre-prompt state
2. **Creates a file on disk** (`subagent_output.go`) simulating what a subagent would write
3. **Builds a main transcript** that contains ONLY a Task tool call (no Write/Edit calls) -- this is the key scenario that was broken before the fix
4. **Includes a tool_result** with `"agentId: sub123abc"` in the format `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs` expects
5. **Creates a subagent transcript** at `<transcriptDir>/<sessionID>/subagents/agent-sub123abc.jsonl` with Write tool calls referencing the created file
6. **Calls `SimulateStop`** and verifies no error occurs
7. **Strategy-specific verification:**
   - **auto-commit**: Verifies a new commit was created on the active branch
   - **manual-commit**: Verifies the shadow branch exists and session state has a non-zero step count

### Key design decisions

- The test manually constructs the tool_result message rather than using `AddTaskToolResult`, because the builder's default format (`"Task completed by agent <id>"`) does not match the `"agentId: <id>"` format that `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs` parses from real Claude Code transcripts.
- Uses `RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv` (not feature branch) since stop hook tests work on any branch.
- The subagent transcript directory follows the exact path convention used by the Stop hook: `<transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/agent-<agentID>.jsonl`.

### Tests added

- `TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges` -- runs for both `auto-commit` and `manual-commit` strategies

### Validation

- The new test passes for both strategies
- Full integration test suite passes
- Full unit test suite passes
- Linting and formatting checks pass

   243→// TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsOverwrittenHooks verifies that EnsureSetup is called
   244→// during user-prompt-submit (start of turn) and reinstalls hooks that were overwritten
   245→// by third-party tools like lefthook. This ensures hooks are in place before any
   246→// mid-turn commits the agent might make.
   247→func TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsOverwrittenHooks(t *testing.T) {
   248→	t.Parallel()
   249→	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   250→		hooksDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "hooks")
   251→		hookNames := strategy.ManagedGitHookNames()
   252→
   253→		// Step 1: First user-prompt-submit installs hooks via EnsureSetup
   254→		session := env.NewSession()
   255→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   256→		if err != nil {
   257→			t.Fatalf("First SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   258→		}
   259→
   260→		// Verify hooks are now installed
   261→		if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalledInDir(env.RepoDir) {
   262→			t.Fatal("hooks should be installed after first SimulateUserPromptSubmit")
   263→		}
   264→
   265→		// Step 2: Overwrite hooks with third-party content (simulating lefthook, husky, etc.)
   266→		for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   267→			hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hookName)
   268→			thirdPartyContent := "#!/bin/sh\n# Third-party hook manager\necho 'Running third-party hook'\n"
   269→			if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(thirdPartyContent), 0o755); err != nil {
   270→				t.Fatalf("failed to overwrite hook %s: %v", hookName, err)
   271→			}
   272→		}
   273→
   274→		// Step 3: Verify hooks are no longer Entire hooks
   275→		if strategy.IsGitHookInstalledInDir(env.RepoDir) {
   276→			t.Fatal("hooks should NOT be detected as Entire hooks after overwrite")
   277→		}
   278→
   279→		// Step 4: Second user-prompt-submit should reinstall hooks
   280→		err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   281→		if err != nil {
   282→			t.Fatalf("Second SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   283→		}
   284→
   285→		// Step 5: Verify hooks are reinstalled
   286→		if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalledInDir(env.RepoDir) {
   287→			t.Error("hooks should be reinstalled after second SimulateUserPromptSubmit")
   288→		}
   289→
   290→		// Step 6: Verify the hooks chain to original hooks (backup should exist)
   291→		for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   292→			backupPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hookName+".pre-entire")
   293→			if _, err := os.Stat(backupPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   294→				t.Errorf("backup hook %s.pre-entire should exist", hookName)
   295→			}
   296→		}
   297→	})
   298→}
   299→
   300→// TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsDeletedHooks verifies that EnsureSetup reinstalls
   301→// hooks that were completely deleted by third-party tools.
   302→func TestUserPromptSubmit_ReinstallsDeletedHooks(t *testing.T) {

   317→			t.Fatal("hooks should be installed after first SimulateUserPromptSubmit")
   318→		}
   319→
   320→		// Step 2: Delete all hooks (simulating aggressive third-party tool)

   130→// This is a regression test for ENT-297 where subagent file modifications were invisible
   131→// to checkpoint detection because ExtractModifiedFiles only looked at the main transcript.
   132→// The fix (ExtractAllModifiedFiles) also reads subagent transcript files.
   133→func TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {
   134→	t.Parallel()
   135→	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
   136→		// Create a session
   137→		session := env.NewSession()
   138→
   139→		// Simulate user prompt submit first (captures pre-prompt state)
   140→		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   141→		if err != nil {
   142→			t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   143→		}
   144→
   145→		// Record initial state for comparison
   146→		commitsBefore := env.GetGitLog()
   147→
   148→		// Create a file on disk (simulating what a subagent would write)
   149→		env.WriteFile("subagent_output.go", "package main\n\nfunc SubagentWork() {}\n")
   150→
   151→		// Build the main transcript manually. The main transcript contains ONLY
   152→		// a Task tool call (no Write/Edit). All file modifications happened in
   153→		// the subagent.
   154→		mainTranscript := NewTranscriptBuilder()
   155→		mainTranscript.AddUserMessage("Create a function in a new file")
   156→		mainTranscript.AddAssistantMessage("I'll delegate this to a subagent.")
   157→
   158→		// Add Task tool use
   159→		taskToolUseID := mainTranscript.AddTaskToolUse("", "Create the function")
   160→
   161→		// Add Task tool result with agentId in the format ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs expects.
   162→		// Note: We cannot use AddTaskToolResult because it produces "Task completed by agent <id>"
   163→		// which doesn't match the "agentId: <id>" format that ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs parses.
   164→		agentID := "sub123abc"
   165→		mainTranscript.messages = append(mainTranscript.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   166→			"uuid": "user-taskresult",
   167→			"type": "user",
   168→			"message": map[string]interface{}{
   169→				"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   170→					{
   171→						"type":        "tool_result",
   172→						"tool_use_id": taskToolUseID,
   173→						"content":     "agentId: " + agentID,
   174→					},
   175→				},
   176→			},
   177→			"timestamp": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
   178→		})
   179→
   180→		mainTranscript.AddAssistantMessage("The subagent completed the task.")
   181→
   182→		// Write the main transcript
   183→		if err := mainTranscript.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   184→			t.Fatalf("failed to write main transcript: %v", err)
   185→		}
   186→
   187→		// Create the subagent transcript file in the directory structure the Stop hook expects:
   188→		// <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/agent-<agentID>.jsonl
   189→		transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(session.TranscriptPath)
   190→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, session.ID, "subagents")
   191→		subagentTranscriptPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, "agent-"+agentID+".jsonl")
   192→
   193→		// Build the subagent transcript with Write tool calls
   194→		subagentTranscript := NewTranscriptBuilder()
   195→		subagentTranscript.AddUserMessage("Create a function in a new file")
   196→		absFilePath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "subagent_output.go")
   197→		toolID := subagentTranscript.AddToolUse("Write", absFilePath, "package main\n\nfunc SubagentWork() {}\n")
   198→		subagentTranscript.AddToolResult(toolID)
   199→		subagentTranscript.AddAssistantMessage("Done, I created the function.")
   200→
   201→		if err := subagentTranscript.WriteToFile(subagentTranscriptPath); err != nil {
   202→			t.Fatalf("failed to write subagent transcript: %v", err)
   203→		}
   204→
   205→		// Simulate stop - this should NOT error and should create a checkpoint
   206→		err = env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath)
   207→		if err != nil {
   208→			t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   209→		}
   210→
   211→		// Verify checkpoint was created based on strategy type
   212→		switch strategyName {
   213→		case strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit:
   214→			// Auto-commit creates a new commit on the active branch
   215→			commitsAfter := env.GetGitLog()
   216→			if len(commitsAfter) <= len(commitsBefore) {
   217→				t.Errorf("auto-commit: expected new commit to be created; commits before=%d, after=%d",
   218→					len(commitsBefore), len(commitsAfter))
   219→			}
   220→
   221→		case strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit:
   222→			// Manual-commit stores checkpoint data on the shadow branch
   223→			shadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchName()
   224→			if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranch) {
   225→				t.Errorf("manual-commit: shadow branch %s should exist after checkpoint", shadowBranch)
   226→			}
   227→
   228→			// Verify session state was updated with checkpoint count
   229→			state, stateErr := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)

Excellent! The test passes. Now let me do a comprehensive review against the spec requirements:

## Code Review: `TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges`

### Spec Requirements vs Implementation

**Requirement 1: Test simulates a Stop hook where the main transcript only has Task tool calls (no Write/Edit)**
- ✅ **MET** - Lines 151-180: Main transcript contains only `AddUserMessage`, `AddAssistantMessage`, `AddTaskToolUse`, and a manually constructed task result. No Write/Edit tool calls.
- The comment on line 151-153 explicitly documents this: "The main transcript contains ONLY a Task tool call (no Write/Edit)"

**Requirement 2: Subagent transcript files exist with Write calls referencing created files**
- ✅ **MET** - Lines 187-203: Creates subagent transcript at the correct location with Write tool use
- Line 197: `AddToolUse("Write", absFilePath, ...)` creates a Write tool call
- Line 149: File is actually created on disk with `env.WriteFile()` to match the Write call

**Requirement 3: Verify checkpoint IS created (not skipped) for both strategies**
- ✅ **MET** - Lines 211-239: Strategy-specific verification
  - **auto-commit**: Lines 213-219 verify a new commit was created
  - **manual-commit**: Lines 221-238 verify shadow branch exists and session state updated with non-zero step count
- Test output confirms checkpoints were created for both strategies

**Requirement 4: Must use `RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv`**
- ✅ **MET** - Line 135: Uses `RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(...) { ... })`

**Requirement 5: Must use `t.Parallel()` at the top level**
- ✅ **MET** - Line 134: `t.Parallel()` called immediately after function declaration

**Requirement 6: Must properly create the subagent transcript at `<transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/agent-<agentID>.jsonl`**
- ✅ **MET** - Lines 187-191: 
  ```go
  transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(session.TranscriptPath)
  subagentsDir := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, session.ID, "subagents")
  subagentTranscriptPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, "agent-"+agentID+".jsonl")
  ```
- The path construction matches the expected format exactly

### Additional Quality Observations

**Strengths:**

1. **Comprehensive Documentation**: Lines 130-132 provide excellent context about the regression being tested (ENT-297) and the fix
2. **Realistic Scenario**: The test accurately simulates a real subagent workflow with proper tool call sequencing
3. **Correct Format Handling**: Lines 161-178 correctly handle the `agentId: <id>` format expected by `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs`, with a helpful comment explaining why the builder helper couldn't be used
4. **Strategy-Specific Assertions**: Lines 211-239 verify the correct behavior for each strategy type
5. **Proper Setup**: Line 139-143 includes `SimulateUserPromptSubmit` to establish pre-prompt state

**Minor Observations:**

1. **No Issues Found**: The implementation is clean and follows all documented patterns from `CLAUDE.md`:
   - Uses parallel tests correctly
   - Uses the integration test helper framework properly
   - Follows the standard test structure seen in other tests in this file

2. **agentID Format Handling** (Lines 161-178): The manual construction of the task result is necessary and well-documented. The comment explains why `AddTaskToolResult()` helper can't be used (format mismatch).

3. **File Path Construction** (Line 196): Uses `filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ...)` to create absolute path, which is correct per CLAUDE.md guidelines about repo root vs cwd.

### Verdict

**✅ ALL SPEC REQUIREMENTS MET**

The implementation fully satisfies all 6 specification requirements with no gaps. The test:
- Correctly simulates the subagent-only modification scenario
- Creates proper transcript structures in the expected locations
- Verifies checkpoint creation for both strategies
- Uses the correct test infrastructure (`RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv`, `t.Parallel()`)
- Is well-documented and follows project conventions

**No issues or recommendations for changes.** The test is production-ready and effectively validates the ENT-297 fix.

{"id":"49f89cc1-2839-4b3c-b9c7-1b3da84c6e7a","identifier":"ENT-297","title":"Subagent file modifications invisible to checkpoint detection","description":"## Problem\n\n`ExtractModifiedFiles` (`agent/claudecode/transcript.go`) only parses the **main agent transcript** for Write/Edit/NotebookEdit tool_use blocks. When subagents (via the `Task` tool) perform file modifications, those tool_use entries live in **separate subagent transcript files** — making them completely invisible to the detection logic.\n\n### Impact\n\n1. **Missing checkpoints (root cause of** [ENT-287](https://linear.app/entirehq/issue/ENT-287/checkpoint-trailer-added-but-checkpoints-not-created-locally-on) **/ GitHub #274)**: In the Stop hook (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:~254`), `totalChanges` is composed of:\n   * `relModifiedFiles` — from transcript parsing (`ExtractModifiedFiles`)\n   * `relNewFiles` / `relDeletedFiles` — from untracked file comparison (`DetectFileChanges`)\n\n   Modifications to **tracked** files are entirely transcript-derived. When subagents do the work, the main transcript only contains `Task` tool calls (not Write/Edit) → `totalChanges = 0` → `SaveChanges` skipped → no shadow branch created → no condensation → **no checkpoint persisted**.\n2. **Misattribution**: Subagent file changes are not attributed to the agent — they appear as \"human\" edits since they're invisible to the transcript-based detection.\n\n### Why this wasn't caught in tests\n\nAll existing tests (e.g., `phase_postcommit_test.go:852`, `phase_transitions_test.go:74`) call `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` or `SimulateStop` before mid-session commits, ensuring the shadow branch always exists. No test exercises the path where only subagents modify files.\n\n### Reproduction\n\n1. Start a session where the main agent delegates all file modifications to subagents (Task tool)\n2. The subagents call Write/Edit to modify tracked files\n3. Stop the session — observe `totalChanges = 0`, \"No files were modified\" message\n4. Make a commit — no `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer, no condensation\n\n### Where the bug lives\n\n| File | Function | Issue |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `agent/claudecode/transcript.go` | `ExtractModifiedFiles` | Only parses main transcript JSONL |\n| `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:~218` | `commitWithMetadata` | `modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)` — only main transcript |\n| `state.go` | `DetectFileChanges` | Only handles new/deleted **untracked** files, not modifications to tracked files |\n| `manual_commit_hooks.go:~1049` | `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` | Same `ExtractModifiedFiles` blind spot |\n\n### Possible approaches\n\n1. **Parse subagent transcripts too**: `ExtractModifiedFiles` could also scan `.entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/*/agent-*.jsonl` files for Write/Edit tool calls\n2. **Filesystem-based detection**: Compare working tree against HEAD (or pre-prompt snapshot) to detect all modifications regardless of source — more robust but different tradeoffs\n3. **Hybrid**: Use transcript parsing for attribution, filesystem diff for completeness\n\n### Related\n\n* ENT-287: Checkpoint trailer added but checkpoints not created locally\n* GitHub #274: `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch never updated\n* ENT-296: Logging improvements (discovered during this investigation)","priority":{"value":1,"name":"Urgent"},"url":"https://linear.app/entirehq/issue/ENT-297/subagent-file-modifications-invisible-to-checkpoint-detection","gitBranchName":"alex/ent-297-subagent-file-modifications-invisible-to-checkpoint","createdAt":"2026-02-13T06:05:07.731Z","updatedAt":"2026-02-13T07:13:05.844Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"dueDate":null,"status":"In This is the foundation for\nfixing ENT-297 where subagent-only file changes were invisible to checkpointing.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEntire-Checkpoint: 732abe6dd3e4","messageHeadline":"Add ExtractAllModifiedFiles to extract files from main agent and suba…","oid":"6a8139e947c59231521cedff629282b112e83916"},{"authoredDate":"2026-02-13T07:04:38Z","authors":[{"email":"alex@entire.io","id":"MDQ6VXNlcjM1MTkzNA==","login":"khaong","name":"Alex Ong"},{"email":"noreply@anthropic.com","id":"MDQ6VXNlcjgxODQ3","login":"claude","name":"Claude Opus 4.6"}],"committedDate":"2026-02-13T07:11:38Z","messageBody":"Replace extractModifiedFiles (main transcript only) with\nclaudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles which also parses subagent\ntranscripts. Also reuses\nthe subagentsDir variable for token usage calculation to avoid\nredefinition.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEntire-Checkpoint: 732abe6dd3e4","messageHeadline":"Update Stop hook to extract modified files from subagent transcripts","oid":"5ea856d64e10fb43c892f462ae5d57799224b3ea"},{"authoredDate":"2026-02-13T07:16:43Z","authors":[{"email":"alex@entire.io","id":"MDQ6VXNlcjM1MTkzNA==","login":"khaong","name":"Alex Ong"},{"email":"noreply@anthropic.com","id":"MDQ6VXNlcjgxODQ3","login":"claude","name":"Claude Opus 4.6"}],"committedDate":"2026-02-13T07:16:43Z","messageBody":"…difications\n\nThe PrepareCommitMsg hook's live transcript check only scanned the main\ntranscript via ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset, missing file changes made\nby subagents spawned via the Task tool. When the main transcript contained\nonly Task calls (no direct Write/Edit), modifiedFiles was empty and the\nfunction returned false -- causing missing checkpoint trailers on commits\nthat included subagent-written files.\n\nAfter the existing main transcript scan, also call\nclaudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles which parses both the main and subagent\ntranscripts, picking up Write/Edit tool calls from subagent files.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEntire-Checkpoint: 732abe6dd3e4","messageHeadline":"Fix sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript to detect subagent file mo…","oid":"32a4280e8e8e265e1fa3b603a8505a48028abe93"},{"authoredDate":"2026-02-13T12:34:45Z","authors":[{"email":"alex@entire.io","id":"MDQ6VXNlcjM1MTkzNA==","login":"khaong","name":"Alex Ong"},{"email":"noreply@anthropic.com","id":"MDQ6VXNlcjgxODQ3","login":"claude","name":"Claude Opus 4.6"}],"committedDate":"2026-02-13T12:34:45Z","messageBody":"…-297)\n\nVerify that the Stop hook creates checkpoints when only subagents\nmodify files. diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go
index de344d7be..06da63377 100644
--- a/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go
@@ -427,3 +427,49 @@ func CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir
 
 	return mainUsage, nil
 }
+
+// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and
+// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
+// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
+// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
+// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
+func ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
+	if transcriptPath == "" {
+		return nil, nil
+	}
+
+	// Parse main transcript once
+	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
+	}
+
+	// Collect modified files from main agent
+	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
+	var files []string
+	for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(transcript) {
+		if !fileSet[f] {
+			fileSet[f] = true
+			files = append(files, f)
+		}
+	}
+
+	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
+	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)
+	for agentID := range agentIDs {
+		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
+		agentTranscript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(agentPath, 0)
+		if err != nil {
+			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
+			continue
+		}
+		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentTranscript) {
+			if !fileSet[f] {
+				fileSet[f] = true
+				files = append(files, f)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return files, nil
+}

+// writeJSONLFile is a test helper that writes JSONL transcript lines to a file.
+func writeJSONLFile(t *testing.T, path string, lines ...string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	var buf strings.Builder
+	for _, line := range lines {
+		buf.WriteString(line)
+		buf.WriteByte('\n')
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(buf.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to write JSONL file %s: %v", path, err)
+	}
+}
+
+// makeWriteToolLine returns a JSONL assistant line with a Write tool_use for the given file.
+func makeWriteToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
+			{
+				"type":  "tool_use",
+				"id":    "toolu_" + uuid,
+				"name":  "Write",
+				"input": map[string]string{"file_path": filePath},
+			},
+		},
+	})
+	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"type":    "assistant",
+		"uuid":    uuid,
+		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
+	})
+	return string(line)
+}
+
+// makeEditToolLine returns a JSONL assistant line with an Edit tool_use for the given file.
+func makeEditToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
+			{
+				"type":  "tool_use",
+				"id":    "toolu_" + uuid,
+				"name":  "Edit",
+				"input": map[string]string{"file_path": filePath},
+			},
+		},
+	})
+	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"type":    "assistant",
+		"uuid":    uuid,
+		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
+	})
+	return string(line)
+}
+
+// makeTaskToolUseLine returns a JSONL assistant line with a Task tool_use (spawning a subagent).
+func makeTaskToolUseLine(t *testing.T, uuid, toolUseID string) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
+			{
+				"type":  "tool_use",
+				"id":    toolUseID,
+				"name":  "Task",
+				"input": map[string]string{"prompt": "do something"},
+			},
+		},
+	})
+	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"type":    "assistant",
+		"uuid":    uuid,
+		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
+	})
+	return string(line)
+}
+
+// makeTaskResultLine returns a JSONL user line with a tool_result containing agentId.
+func makeTaskResultLine(t *testing.T, uuid, toolUseID, agentID string) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
+			{
+				"type":        "tool_result",
+				"tool_use_id": toolUseID,
+				"content":     "agentId: " + agentID,
+			},
+		},
+	})
+	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
+		"type":    "user",
+		"uuid":    uuid,
+		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
+	})
+	return string(line)
+}
+
+func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_IncludesSubagentFiles(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
+	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
+
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Main transcript: Write to main.go + Task call spawning subagent "sub1"
+	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
+		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/main.go"),
+		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
+		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
+	)
+
+	// Subagent transcript: Write to helper.go + Edit to utils.go
+	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
+		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/helper.go"),
+		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/utils.go"),
+	)
+
+	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(files) != 3 {
+		t.Errorf("expected 3 files, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
+	}
+
+	wantFiles := map[string]bool{
+		"/repo/main.go":   true,
+		"/repo/helper.go": true,
+		"/repo/utils.go":  true,
+	}
+	for _, f := range files {
+		if !wantFiles[f] {
+			t.Errorf("unexpected file %q in result", f)
+		}
+		delete(wantFiles, f)
+	}
+	for f := range wantFiles {
+		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_DeduplicatesAcrossAgents(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
+	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
+
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Main transcript: Write to shared.go + Task call
+	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
+		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/shared.go"),
+		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
+		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
+	)
+
+	// Subagent transcript: Also modifies shared.go (same file as main)
+	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
+		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/shared.go"),
+	)
+
+	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(files) != 1 {
+		t.Errorf("expected 1 file (deduplicated), got %d: %v", len(files), files)
+	}
+	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/shared.go" {
+		t.Errorf("expected /repo/shared.go, got %q", files[0])
+	}
+}
+
+func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_NoSubagents(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()

+

+
+	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, tmpDir+"/nonexistent")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(files) != 1 {
+		t.Errorf("expected 1 file, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
+	}
+	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/solo.go" {
+		t.Errorf("expected /repo/solo.go, got %q", files[0])
+	}
+}
+
+func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
+	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
+
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Main transcript: ONLY a Task call, no direct file modifications
+	// This is the key bug scenario - if we only look at the main transcript,
+	// we miss all the subagent's file changes entirely.
+	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
+		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a1", "toolu_task1"),
+		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
+	)
+
+	// Subagent transcript: Write to two files
+	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
+		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/subagent_file1.go"),
+		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/subagent_file2.go"),
+	)
+
+	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	if len(files) != 2 {
+		t.Errorf("expected 2 files from subagent, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
+	}
+
+	wantFiles := map[string]bool{
+		"/repo/subagent_file1.go": true,
+		"/repo/subagent_file2.go": true,
+	}
+	for _, f := range files {
+		if !wantFiles[f] {
+			t.Errorf("unexpected file %q in result", f)
+		}
+		delete(wantFiles, f)
+	}
+	for f := range wantFiles {
+		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
+	}
+}

+// TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges tests that the stop hook correctly
+// creates a checkpoint when ONLY subagents (spawned via Task tool) modify files.
+// This is a regression test for ENT-297 where subagent file modifications were invisible
+// to checkpoint detection because ExtractModifiedFiles only looked at the main transcript.
+// The fix (ExtractAllModifiedFiles) also reads subagent transcript files.
+func TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+	RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
+		// Create a session
+		session := env.NewSession()
+
+		// Simulate user prompt submit first (captures pre-prompt state)
+		err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		// Record initial state for comparison
+		commitsBefore := env.GetGitLog()
+
+		// Create a file on disk (simulating what a subagent would write)
+		env.WriteFile("subagent_output.go", "package main\n\nfunc SubagentWork() {}\n")
+
+		// Build the main transcript manually. The main transcript contains ONLY
+		// a Task tool call (no Write/Edit). All file modifications happened in
+		// the subagent.
+		mainTranscript := NewTranscriptBuilder()
+		mainTranscript.AddUserMessage("Create a function in a new file")
+		mainTranscript.AddAssistantMessage("I'll delegate this to a subagent.")
+
+		// Add Task tool use
+		taskToolUseID := mainTranscript.AddTaskToolUse("", "Create the function")
+
+		// Add Task tool result with agentId in the format ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs expects.
+		// Note: We cannot use AddTaskToolResult because it produces "Task completed by agent <id>"
+		// which doesn't match the "agentId: <id>" format that ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs parses.
+		agentID := "sub123abc"
+		mainTranscript.messages = append(mainTranscript.messages, map[string]interface{}{
+			"uuid": "user-taskresult",
+			"type": "user",
+			"message": map[string]interface{}{
+				"content": []map[string]interface{}{
+					{
+						"type":        "tool_result",
+						"tool_use_id": taskToolUseID,
+						"content":     "agentId: " + agentID,
+					},
+				},
+			},
+			"timestamp": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
+		})
+
+		mainTranscript.AddAssistantMessage("The subagent completed the task.")
+
+		// Write the main transcript
+		if err := mainTranscript.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to write main transcript: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		// Create the subagent transcript file in the directory structure the Stop hook expects:
+		// <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/agent-<agentID>.jsonl
+		transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(session.TranscriptPath)
+		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, session.ID, "subagents")
+		subagentTranscriptPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, "agent-"+agentID+".jsonl")
+
+		// Build the subagent transcript with Write tool calls
+		subagentTranscript := NewTranscriptBuilder()
+		subagentTranscript.AddUserMessage("Create a function in a new file")
+		absFilePath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "subagent_output.go")
+		toolID := subagentTranscript.AddToolUse("Write", absFilePath, "package main\n\nfunc SubagentWork() {}\n")
+		subagentTranscript.AddToolResult(toolID)
+		subagentTranscript.AddAssistantMessage("Done, I created the function.")
+
+		if err := subagentTranscript.WriteToFile(subagentTranscriptPath); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to write subagent transcript: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		// Simulate stop - this should NOT error and should create a checkpoint
+		err = env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		// Verify checkpoint was created based on strategy type
+		switch strategyName {
+		case strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit:
+			// Auto-commit creates a new commit on the active branch
+			commitsAfter := env.GetGitLog()
+			if len(commitsAfter) <= len(commitsBefore) {
+				t.Errorf("auto-commit: expected new commit to be created; commits before=%d, after=%d",
+					len(commitsBefore), len(commitsAfter))
+			}
+
+		case strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit:
+			// Manual-commit stores checkpoint data on the shadow branch
+			shadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchName()
+			if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranch) {
+				t.Errorf("manual-commit: shadow branch %s should exist after checkpoint", shadowBranch)
+			}
+
+			// Verify session state was updated with checkpoint count
+			state, stateErr := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
+			if stateErr != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("failed to get session state: %v", stateErr)
+			}
+			if state == nil {
+				t.Fatal("manual-commit: session state should exist after checkpoint")
+			}
+			if state.StepCount == 0 {
+				t.Error("manual-commit: session state should have non-zero step count")
+			}
+		}
+	})
+}
+

+// TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_IncludesSubagentFiles verifies
+// that sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript detects file modifications made by
+// subagents spawned via the Task tool.
+//
+// Bug: The function only scans the main transcript via ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset,
+// which misses file changes made by subagents. When the main transcript contains only
+// a Task tool call (no direct Write/Edit), modifiedFiles is empty and the function
+// returns false -- even though the subagent wrote files that are staged for commit.
+func TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_IncludesSubagentFiles(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+
+	// Create a file that we'll stage
+	srcDir := filepath.Join(dir, "src")
+	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(srcDir, 0o755))
+	testFile := filepath.Join(srcDir, "feature.go")
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package src\n"), 0o644))
+
+	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	_, err = wt.Add("src/feature.go")
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Get the resolved worktree path first — on macOS, t.TempDir() returns /var/...
+	// but git resolves symlinks to /private/var/... . Claude Code uses the resolved
+	// path in its transcript, so we must too.
+	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Create a main transcript that ONLY has a Task tool call — no direct Write/Edit.
+	// The assistant invokes Task, and the user line returns the tool_result with agentId.
+	const modelSessionID = "model-session-sub"
+	transcriptDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".entire", "metadata")
+	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(transcriptDir, 0o755))
+
+	mainTranscript := `{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_task1","name":"Task","input":{"prompt":"implement feature"}}]}}
+{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_task1","content":"agentId: sub123"}]}}
+`
+	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, "transcript.jsonl")
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(mainTranscript), 0o644))
+
+	// Create the subagent transcript with a Write tool_use targeting the staged file.
+	// Path: <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/agent-sub123.jsonl
+	absFeaturePath := filepath.Join(worktreePath, "src", "feature.go")
+	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, modelSessionID, "subagents")
+	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755))
+
+	subagentTranscript := `{"type":"assistant","uuid":"sa1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_write1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"` + absFeaturePath + `","content":"package src\n"}}]}}
+`
+	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
+		filepath.Join(subagentsDir, "agent-sub123.jsonl"),
+		[]byte(subagentTranscript), 0o644,
+	))
+
+	// Create session state: no shadow branch, transcript has only Task calls,
+	// agent type is Claude Code so the subagent path resolution works
+	now := time.Now()
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	state := &SessionState{
+		SessionID:                 modelSessionID,
+		BaseCommit:                head.Hash().String(),
+		WorktreePath:              worktreePath,
+		WorktreeID:                worktreeID,
+		StartedAt:                 now,
+		Phase:                     session.PhaseActive,
+		LastInteractionTime:       &now,
+		AgentType:                 agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode,
+		TranscriptPath:            transcriptPath,
+		CheckpointTranscriptStart: 0,
+	}
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	// Call sessionHasNewContent — should fall through to live transcript check
+	// since there's no shadow branch, and should detect subagent file modifications
+	hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	assert.True(t, hasNew,
+		"sessionHasNewContent should return true when subagent transcript "+
+			"contains Write tool calls for staged files, even though the main "+
+			"transcript has no direct file modifications")
+}
+
