## Code Review: PR #323 — Fix subagent file modifications invisible to checkpoint detection

**4 commits, +539/-13, 9 files changed**

---

### Overview

Fixes ENT-297 where checkpoints were skipped when only subagents (via the Task tool) modified files. The main transcript only contained `Task` tool calls, so `ExtractModifiedFiles` returned nothing, `totalChanges = 0`, and the checkpoint was skipped.

The fix adds `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` that also reads subagent transcript files, and wires it into two code paths: the Stop hook and mid-session commit detection.

---

### Code Correctness

**`ExtractAllModifiedFiles` (`transcript.go:429-475`)**
- Follows the exact same pattern as `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` — parse main, extract agent IDs, read subagent transcripts, merge. Consistent and correct.
- Gracefully continues on subagent transcript read errors (`continue`). Good — subagent files may not exist yet or may be cleaned up.
- Deduplication via `map[string]bool` with order-preserving append. Correct.

**Stop hook (`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:224-232`)**
- Uses `input.SessionID` (model session ID) for the subagent path, not `sessionID` which may be overridden to `unknownSessionID`. Correct and important.
- Falls back to `extractModifiedFiles(transcript)` on error. Sensible graceful degradation.
- Reuses `subagentsDir` for the existing `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` call, removing a duplicate declaration and renaming `err` to `tokenErr`. Clean.

**Mid-session commit detection (`manual_commit_hooks.go:1011-1021`)**
- Guards on `state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode`. Correct — only Claude Code has subagent transcripts.
- Uses `len(allFiles) > len(modifiedFiles)` to only replace if the subagent-aware result is larger. Subtle but correct — if `ExtractAllModifiedFiles` returns fewer (shouldn't happen in practice), we keep the main-only result.

---

### Potential Issues

1. **`AddTaskToolResult` format mismatch** — The integration test notes that `AddTaskToolResult` produces `"Task completed by agent <id>"` but `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs` expects `"agentId: <id>"`. This means the `TranscriptBuilder.AddTaskToolResult` helper is producing transcripts that don't match real Claude Code behavior. Not a bug in this PR, but worth a follow-up to fix the helper so future tests don't silently produce unrealistic transcripts.

2. **Nolint comment rewording** — The `//nolint:ireturn` comments in `auto_commit.go`, `manual_commit.go`, and `registry.go` were changed from `"already present in codebase"` to `"factory returns interface by design"` / `"registry returns interface by design"`. These are cosmetic improvements, fine to keep but slightly unrelated to the bug fix.

3. **Import tab fix** in `hooks_test.go` line 10 — spaces changed to tabs in the import. Cosmetic, fine.

---

### Test Coverage

- **Unit tests (4)**: `IncludesSubagentFiles`, `DeduplicatesAcrossAgents`, `NoSubagents`, `SubagentOnlyChanges` — covers all key scenarios
- **Unit test for mid-session detection**: `TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_IncludesSubagentFiles` — correctly uses `t.Chdir` (not parallel) since it modifies process-global state
- **Integration test**: `TestHookRunner_SimulateStop_SubagentOnlyChanges` — end-to-end validation for both strategies
- Test helpers (`writeJSONLFile`, `makeWriteToolLine`, etc.) are well-structured and reusable

---

### Verdict

Clean, well-tested fix that follows existing patterns. The approach is the right one — extending transcript parsing rather than adding a new detection mechanism. The graceful fallback on errors is good defensive coding. Ready to merge after CI passes.

One minor follow-up suggestion: fix `TranscriptBuilder.AddTaskToolResult` to produce the `"agentId: <id>"` format that matches real Claude Code output.

## Consolidated Review Feedback

### Actionable in this PR

| # | Source | File | Issue | Action |
|---|--------|------|-------|--------|
| 1 | Copilot #5 | `manual_commit_hooks.go:1020` | Silent error swallowing — `extractErr` is ignored with no logging | **Fix**: Add `logging.Debug` when `extractErr != nil` |
| 2 | Both | `integration_test/hooks_test.go:178` | Test reaches into `TranscriptBuilder.messages` internal slice | **Fix**: Update `AddTaskToolResult` to produce the correct `"agentId: <id>"` format, then use the builder API |

### Not actionable in this PR (follow-ups)

| # | Source | Issue | Why defer |
|---|--------|-------|-----------|
| 3 | Copilot #1 | `parseTranscriptFromLine` uses `bufio.Scanner` with 10MB limit | Pre-existing — affects `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` and all other callers equally. Not introduced by this PR. |
| 4 | Copilot #2 | `startLine > 0` misses subagent IDs spawned in earlier turns | Pre-existing limitation shared with `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` (same pattern). Fixing this requires a broader change to extract agent IDs from line 0 always. |
| 5 | Copilot #4 | Fallback to main-only on error could reintroduce ENT-297 | **Disagree** — failing the entire Stop hook would be worse than a partial result. The error is already printed to stderr. Graceful degradation is the right choice for a hook that runs in CI/user workflows. |

---

**My recommendation:** Fix items #1 and #2 (both are small, clean improvements), then reply to all 5 Copilot threads. Items #3 and #4 could be tracked as follow-up issues if you think they're worth pursuing. Item #5 I'd push back on.

```json
  "command": "gh issue create --title \"bufio.Scanner 10MB token limit may truncate large transcript lines\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Problem\n\n`parseTranscriptFromLine` in `agent/claudecode/transcript.go` uses `bufio.Scanner` with a 10MB max token size. Claude Code `tool_use` JSONL lines can embed large file contents, and if a single line exceeds that limit, parsing will error.\n\nThis affects all callers:\n- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles`\n- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage`\n- Other transcript parsing functions\n\n## Impact\n\nIf a transcript line exceeds 10MB, file extraction and token usage calculation will fail. In `ExtractAllModifiedFiles`, the error triggers a fallback to main-transcript-only extraction, potentially missing subagent files.\n\n## Suggested Fix\n\nConsider switching to a `bufio.Reader`-based parser (like `ClaudeCodeAgent.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset`) that doesn't have fixed token limits.\n\n## Context\n\nIdentified during review of #323 (ENT-297 fix).\nEOF\n)\" --label \"Bug\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Create GitHub issue for bufio.Scanner limit"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "gh issue create --title \"ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs misses subagents spawned before startLine offset\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Problem\n\n`ExtractAllModifiedFiles` and `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` both extract spawned agent IDs from the transcript slice starting at `startLine`. When `startLine > 0` (i.e., after a checkpoint), subagents spawned in earlier turns are not discovered.\n\nIf those earlier-spawned subagents continue writing to their transcript files in later turns, their file modifications will be missed.\n\n## Impact\n\nAfter a checkpoint is saved (advancing `CheckpointTranscriptStart`), modifications by previously-spawned subagents become invisible to both file extraction and token usage calculation.\n\n## Suggested Fix\n\nExtract agent IDs from the full transcript (`startLine=0`) while still limiting main-agent modified file extraction to `startLine`. This ensures all subagent transcripts are scanned regardless of when the agent was spawned.\n\n## Context\n\nIdentified during review of #323 (ENT-297 fix). This is a pre-existing limitation shared by `CalculateTotalTokenUsage`.\nEOF\n)\" --label \"Bug\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Create GitHub issue for startLine agent ID miss"
}
```

All done. Here's the summary:

**Fixes committed and pushed** (41f0249e):
- **#1**: Added `logging.Debug` in `manual_commit_hooks.go` when `extractErr != nil`
- **#2**: Fixed `AddTaskToolResult` to produce `"agentId: <id>"` format + updated integration test to use the builder API

**Follow-up issues created:**
- #328 — bufio.Scanner 10MB token limit
- #329 — startLine > 0 misses earlier subagent IDs

**All 5 Copilot threads replied to:**
- #1, #2: Acknowledged as pre-existing, tracked in follow-up issues
- #3: Fixed in commit
- #4: Pushed back (graceful degradation is intentional)
- #5: Fixed in commit
